# Verified gap map (run wf_eac5a5ef-3a0, 2026-10-30)

Each domain: mapper items (spec vs code) with the verifier corrections applied. Status: built, partial, missing, diverges, not-specced.

## api-auth-security

Today's API is a clean, small Express 5 skeleton (about 3,300 server lines) with sound per-user ownership scoping, Better Auth email+OTP+Google, a working extension bearer flow, a strict job-URL parser and Stripe signature checks. Almost none of the 05-api hardening layer exists: no route registry, requestId, originGuard, session kinds (fresh/nocache/admin), DTO serializers, Postgres rate limiter, admin surface, idempotency keys, cursors, upload route, data export or consent capture. The web reaches the API through a same-origin Next rewrite (web/next.config.ts:25), so cookies are host-only on the Vercel domain and CSRF exposure is low; that same hop makes req.ip the Vercel egress address, so the in-memory IP limiters throttle every web user together. Why sign-in likely fails on staging: (1) env.ts:13-15 requires BETTER_AUTH_URL and WEB_ORIGIN with no default, so 'unset' must really be a placeholder or localhost value; (2) the browser's Origin must be in Better Auth trustedOrigins (auth.ts:32) or every POST /api/auth/* is rejected, including email sign-in; (3) BETTER_AUTH_URL must be the WEB origin because Google's callback has to return through the rewrite to the host that holds the state cookie, yet 05-api.md:397 says to use the API origin (contradicts server/.env.example:10-12); (4) Vercel's API_ORIGIN falls back to http://localhost:4000 (next.config.ts:4) and nothing fails the production build; (5) Apple, GitHub and LinkedIn are not configured server-side (auth.ts:42-45) and are not in the spec. Highest-risk code findings: spoofable and per-instance Better Auth rate limits, global IP throttling via the Vercel hop (a one-client lockout), an unauthenticated 50 MB parse before the inbound-email token check, and unvalidated Lever/Ashby apply URLs that the cloud applier navigates to and the web renders as hrefs.

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] Staging sign-in chain has four independent single points of failure: next.config.ts:4 (localhost fallback); env.ts:13-18; auth.ts:29-32,42-45; .env.example:10-13. Fix: One checklist run: Vercel API_ORIGIN set at build; BETTER_AUTH_URL and WEB_ORIGIN both equal the Vercel origin; Google redirect URI https://<web>/api/auth/callback/google; EXTENSION_ORIGIN for the extension. Add a production guard that fails the web build when API_ORIGIN is missing and a smoke script that signs up through the Vercel URL.
- [high/confirmed] 05-api tells builders to set BETTER_AUTH_URL to the API origin, which breaks OAuth through the rewrite: 05-api.md:397,411 vs .env.example:10-12; 06-infrastructure.md:573 defers to S-B1. Fix: Settle now, not at P1 exit: BETTER_AUTH_URL = web origin, and document that API_PUBLIC_ORIGIN (emailed link posts) is a separate variable.
- [high/confirmed] Rate limiting is in-memory, per instance, and keyed on the Vercel egress IP: middleware/rate-limit.ts:1-20; routes/index.ts:20; app.ts:22; contact.ts:12. Fix: Short term: skip apiLimiter for authenticated requests and key anonymous ones on the real client IP; medium term the Postgres limiter (item 24). Do not scale api past one replica before this.
- [medium] Hand-mounted routes plus PRD R1 to R4 on today's Express will outpace the registry refactor: routes/index.ts:17-35; PRD D7. Fix: Add the authorization matrix test and a route-list completeness test first (S), so new R1 to R4 routes are covered before the registry exists.
- [medium] Extension cannot be connected from production: extension/manifest.json:30-32 (localhost only); build.mjs:7 default API_BASE is the web domain; spec OQ8. Fix: Manifest-only store release before paid beta; decide whether the extension talks to the web domain (rewrite, shared IP limiter) or to Railway directly, then set EXTENSION_ORIGIN accordingly.
- [medium] Better Auth drift and unpinned auth stack: server/package.json:20 ^1.7.7; node_modules 1.7.7 vs spec 1.7.10; ci.yml has no audit or SAST. Fix: Pin exactly, run spike S-B1 against the pinned version, add npm audit/osv-scanner and gitleaks to CI.
- [medium] Public Railway API origin is reachable directly, bypassing Vercel: spec relies on Vercel hop count and a spoofable x-vercel-id (05-api 5.5); Cloudflare lock only as a later trigger (06 line 198). Fix: Add a shared-secret header or private-networking path for the Vercel hop, or accept direct access and make every limiter key on the right-most trusted address.
- [low] Stripe webhook does a network call inside a DB transaction, unmetered: routes/webhooks.ts:37-60 (own ponytail note at :36). Fix: Keep until P2 store-then-process; add webhook_ip limiter now.
- [medium] Sign-up abuse and OTP mail cost: auth.ts:69,74-89,106. Fix: Per-email and per-IP caps on send-verification-otp and sign-up (items 28, 55); optional Turnstile on own forms.

**Security findings**

- [high/refuted] Better Auth rate limits are in-memory and keyed on a forgeable first X-Forwarded-For entry: server/src/lib/auth.ts:69; app.ts:30 mounted before any other limiter; spec 5.1 claims ipAddressHeaders is 'aligned with clientIp()' but Better Auth reads the first entry while clientIp reads from the right. Fix: Callers of the public Railway URL can rotate a fake XFF to evade sign-in, OTP and sign-up limits (credential stuffing, OTP mail bombing). Add per-email limits in hooks.before, set Better Auth's IP resolution to the trusted right-hand entry, and lock the origin to Vercel.
- [high/uncertain] One client can lock out the whole site: 120 req/min and 5 contact posts per hour are shared across every web user: app.ts:22 trust proxy 1; routes/index.ts:20; rate-limit.ts:7-20; web proxies all browsers through Vercel (next.config.ts:25). Fix: Skip IP limiting for signed-in traffic; resolve the real client IP for anonymous traffic (item 23) or limit per user and per session.
- [high/confirmed] Unauthenticated 50 MB JSON body is parsed before the inbound-email token is checked, and webhooks have no limiter: routes/webhooks.ts:71-73; app.ts:31 mounted ahead of apiLimiter. Fix: Verify the secret (path segment) before parsing, cap unauthenticated bodies at a few KB, add webhook_ip limiting; move the secret out of the query string as spec 14.3 requires.
- [high/confirmed] Unvalidated apply URL from Lever and Ashby feeds the cloud applier's browser and the web UI (SSRF and javascript: link): services/ats/lever.ts:8,34; ashby.ts:15,49; jobs/cloud-apply.ts:59; web application-detail.tsx:66, match-card.tsx:64, queue-list.tsx:247. Fix: Allow-list scheme https and the five ATS hosts when storing apply_url and again in the applier; add the applier egress guard; render links only after a scheme check.
- [medium] Whole database rows returned to clients, including Stripe ids, raw job payloads and resume source text: routes/me.ts:19; routes/applications.ts:54; routes/profile.ts:31,63; routes/credits.ts:18. Fix: Explicit serializers before P2 adds sensitive columns (item 32). Own-data only today, so not exploitable cross-user.
- [medium] Raw error objects are logged; Drizzle errors can embed SQL parameters (resume text, emails): middleware/error-handler.ts:36; logger.ts redact list has no err.* or text keys. Fix: Log err.code and a scrubbed message only; add the spec's body, text and answers redaction keys and a pii-scan test.
- [medium] Resume source text and inbound reply bodies stored in plaintext; full bodies kept, no 16 KB truncation: db/schema/profile.ts:34; routes/webhooks.ts:79. Fix: Truncate inbound bodies to 16 KB now; plan column encryption (P6) before beta data accumulates.
- [low] Cookie-authenticated requests have no Origin guard; protection is SameSite=Lax alone outside /api/auth: app.ts:25-36; no originGuard. Fix: Add originGuard, and make sure a request carrying both a cookie and a junk Bearer header cannot skip the check while authenticating by cookie (verify in S-B1).
- [medium] Extension outcome reports are trusted: routes/extension.ts:31-34; services/submissions.ts:49-72. Fix: Cross-check against forwarded confirmation emails and sample audits; require lease/claim (P2); alert on failed-after-confirmation patterns.
- [medium] Account-linking behaviour with four providers is unspecified; pre-hijack path possible: auth.ts has no account.accountLinking policy; web sign-in-methods.tsx offers link/unlink. Fix: Set trusted providers explicitly, accept only verified provider emails, delete or neutralise unverified credential accounts on social link, and test in S-B1.
- [low] Profile and resume URL fields accept javascript: and other schemes: routes/profile.ts:17-19 (z.url()); shared/src/schemas.ts resumeContactSchema.links; web contact-details.tsx:55 renders href. Fix: Restrict to http(s) in zod and at render.
- [low] No CSP on the web app; no secret scanning, SAST or audit in CI: web/next.config.ts:28-36; .github/workflows/ci.yml. Fix: Add a nonce CSP once inline scripts (MOTION_HEAD_SCRIPT, JSON-LD) are accounted for; add gitleaks, Semgrep and osv-scanner.
- [medium] Free credit grant per verified signup invites farming: auth.ts:106; no sign-up cap. Fix: Per-IP daily cap, normalised email hash (strip plus-tags), optional Turnstile on sign-up (own site, not an applier CAPTCHA).

**Spec gaps**

- 05-api 5.1 sets Better Auth baseURL to the public API origin, which is unbuildable behind the Vercel rewrite for OAuth (state cookie host mismatch) and contradicts server/.env.example and the cookie model in 5.2. 06 line 573 defers the choice to S-B1.
- 05-api covers only Google. The UI and settings page show Apple, GitHub and LinkedIn; no provider config, env vars, account-linking policy or Apple form_post/trustedOrigins rule is specified, and 9.3 omits list-accounts, link-social and unlink-account which the web calls.
- 05-api 5.1 says ipAddress.ipAddressHeaders ['x-forwarded-for'] is 'aligned with clientIp()', but Better Auth takes the first entry while clientIp() walks from the right. The Better Auth limiter and the own limiters will disagree and the first is forgeable.
- The Vercel to Railway hop is unauthenticated: the spec trusts x-vercel-id and leaves the public Railway origin open until a later Cloudflare trigger. No mechanism is given to make direct access harmless.
- originGuard step 1 skips the check whenever an Authorization: Bearer header is present; the spec does not state that such a request must then authenticate only by bearer (a cookie plus junk bearer must not pass).
- 05-api 12.3 chooses a raw-body upload route (no multipart); the resume-suite PRD API table specifies multipart POST /profile/imports. The 5 MB cap through the Vercel rewrite is unmeasured (S-INF2).
- Spec says no Redis and Postgres-only rate limits, yet BullMQ and Redis run until P7 and today's limits are in-memory; the interim policy (P0 to P1) for multi-replica limits is not stated.
- Admin access path is a 30-day-class bearer token for an email+password owner account with no MFA or hardware key; 06 requires hardware keys for infrastructure but not for the API admin surface.
- Data subject rights: export is an operator script with a 30-day SLA (06 line 1462), no self-serve route and no consent-record schema; DPDP consent per purpose and grievance contact have no API or table.
- No bot or abuse control for sign-up and the public contact form beyond IP limits that fail behind NAT or the Vercel hop; the 25-credit grant is not tied to a normalised email hash until P2 (spec 5.1 mentions normalized_email_hash).
- Extension API base: spec F-5 says the extension talks to Railway directly, but extension/build.mjs:7 defaults to the web domain and manifest host_permissions lists no API host; hop counts and CORS then differ from 5.5.
- Spec 4.6 exempts /api/auth from the error envelope but gives no rule for 429s from Better Auth's own limiter versus the custom hook, or for 403 'Invalid origin' responses that the web would show as a generic failure.

**Open questions**

- What are the actual values on staging: Vercel API_ORIGIN, sd1-api BETTER_AUTH_URL and WEB_ORIGIN, and is the web served from a custom domain or *.vercel.app? (Decides the Google redirect URI and cookie host.)
- Approve overriding 05-api 5.1: BETTER_AUTH_URL = web origin, with API_PUBLIC_ORIGIN as a separate variable?
- Which sign-in providers must work at paid beta: Google only, or all four? Is there an Apple Developer account, a GitHub OAuth app and a LinkedIn app, and who owns Apple key rotation?
- Should the extension call the web domain (through the rewrite) or Railway directly? Who ships the manifest-only store release, and when?
- Do Vercel preview deployments need working sign-in (wildcard trusted origins), or is production plus localhost enough?
- Accept locking the Railway API origin to Vercel (shared-secret header or Cloudflare) before beta, or live with direct access and right-most-IP limiting?
- Admin access: acceptable that the owner's ordinary email+password account with a 24-hour fresh session is the only gate, or add MFA?
- Is a self-serve data export and a stored consent record required before launch, or is the operator script at P6 enough for DPDP/GDPR? Who is the named grievance contact?
- May sign-up and the contact form use Cloudflare Turnstile (own pages, not the applier)? Is 25 free credits per verified email an acceptable abuse exposure without it?
- Should PRD R1 to R4 routes ship on today's Express before the registry exists (D7), with the registry and authorization matrix retrofitted afterwards?
- Retention and erasure of contact_messages, session IP/user-agent and Stripe customer records on account deletion: delete, anonymise or keep for a stated period?

## applications

Today's application lifecycle is the phase-A skeleton, not the ADR-001 design. It has one BullMQ tailor job, a conditional approve with an advisory lock, one conditional cloud claim, a Playwright run that injects the frozen filler, and a single transactional recordSubmission. Exactly-once charging works: the unique ledger index (server/drizzle/0000_init.sql:255) plus ON CONFLICT (server/src/services/credits.ts:44-51) is pinned by tests. Everything else the spec calls required is missing or built differently: no draft_state, verifier, PDF render, form questions, submission_attempts, leases, sweepers, settlement, failure codes, resend, events, Autopilot or ops flags. The biggest live problems are in what ships today. (1) A cloud run that crashes or is redelivered leaves the application in 'submitting' forever with its credit reserved, and no code or action path moves it on (server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:36-38, server/src/services/submissions.ts:52-59). (2) Any exception after the submit click, including the navigation that normally follows a submit, is recorded as 'failed' with the raw error text, so a sent application can be recorded as unsent and freely resent (cloud-apply.ts:67-70). (3) The frozen filler reads only payload.answers and resumePdfUrl (extension/src/filler.ts:33,40), and the server never supplies a PDF, so tailored resume lines and cover letters never reach an employer today. (4) The applier has no egress guard, host allowlist, least-privilege role or kill switch, while Pro and Max users can already trigger it. The specs are mostly buildable and consistent with each other. Material conflicts are with the resume-suite PRD (filler file name, snapshot timing, event shape) and with the frozen filler (cover letters and resume lines cannot be delivered).

**Risks**

- [critical/confirmed] Cloud application stuck in 'submitting' forever with its credit reserved: Claim moves approved to submitting (server/src/services/submissions.ts:52-59); a crash, OOM kill or BullMQ stalled redelivery then hits the status check and skips (server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:36); no sweeper or timeout exists; availableCreditsNow counts submitting as in flight (server/src/services/credits.ts:19); withdraw allows only draft/approved (routes/applications.ts:144). Fix: Interim: add a minute-level SQL sweep that moves submitting rows older than 10 minutes to failed with an 'unconfirmed' note, or ship applications-74 first. Real fix is submission_attempts plus leaseSweeper (P2).
- [critical/confirmed] Sent applications recorded as failed and freely resendable (post-click exception treated as plain failure): Filler clicks submit then waits up to 20 s in-page (extension/src/filler.ts:64-67); Greenhouse/Lever confirmations are navigations (extension/src/ats/greenhouse.ts:15, lever.ts:17), which destroys the injected context and rejects page.evaluate; the catch records failed with err.message (server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:67-70). Fix: Treat any error after the run reaches the filler call as ambiguous (needs_check, acknowledgement before resend) until submit_seen interception is proven; verify with a throwaway posting (S-AP2).
- [critical/confirmed] Cloud applier live for Pro/Max with no egress guard, host allowlist, kill switch or credential isolation: cloud-apply.ts:21,56,59 (bypassCSP, unrestricted goto of DB-stored apply_url, no proxy); applier shares full env and DB credentials (server/src/lib/env.ts, railway/applier.json); no pause flag. Fix: Before any real paying cloud user: allowlist hosts at the Playwright route layer, restrict DB role and env for the applier, add a pause flag, or disable the cloud channel on staging and production.
- [high/confirmed] Tailored resume and cover letter never reach the employer: Filler uses only payload.answers and resumePdfUrl (extension/src/filler.ts:33-45); queue never supplies resumePdfUrl (routes/extension.ts:14-22); cloud applier attaches no file (cloud-apply.ts:60-64); required-field check skips file inputs (filler.ts:56-58). Fix: Product and owner decision on the frozen filler (see open questions); deliver PDF via setInputFiles in the cloud and presigned URL for the extension; deliver cover letters as an answer entry or attachment.
- [high/confirmed] Model-written resume lines pass an ids-only truth lock; invented numbers, employers or scope are not checked: server/src/services/tailor.ts:19-35,54,73 check citation ids only; inventedDetails (server/src/services/invented-details.ts:12) is applied to answers and letters but not lines; no verifier agent (registry.ts). Fix: Apply checkClaims to tailored and PATCHed lines now (S effort) and land claim-verifier in P3 before Autopilot or PDF render goes live.
- [high/confirmed] Approval lacks readiness, freshness and cap checks; ready is inferred from non-empty JSON: server/src/services/submissions.ts:30-42. Fix: Add draft_state and the human-path skip list (applications-37) before enabling auto-approve; at minimum recheck closed_at and company cap at approval.
- [high/confirmed] Lost enqueue after approval or queueing strands work with no reconciler: routes/applications.ts:93-95 and routes/matches.ts:57-59 enqueue after commit; BullMQ attempts 3 then removeOnFail 7 days (jobs/queues.ts:36-42). Fix: Add a 5-minute reconcile for approved cloud rows without a live job and pending drafts without lines, or move to the DBOS enqueue-in-transaction design (P2).
- [medium] Receipts store every filled value, including EEO/visa/salary answers, indefinitely: server/src/services/submissions.ts:76-83; no scrub job. Fix: Implement the 7-day scrub for sensitive answers (applications-84) and redact confirmation text; decide retention with counsel.
- [medium] Manual extension failures are not reported, so credits stay reserved until return-to-draft (14 days): extension/src/background.ts:38-40 reports failed only for auto-submit; no return-to-draft job. Fix: Ship return-to-draft with P2 and show reserved credits in the web UI.
- [medium] Spec worst-case prepare time (about 660 s) exceeds its own 600 s workflow timeout; delayed Autopilot enqueues may time out before they run: 04 section 5.3 worst case; 07 section 2 timeoutMs 480000 with delaySeconds up to 10 h and a [verify] on when DBOS starts the clock. Fix: Close the Railway half of spike S-D1 before building applications-49 and applications-55; lower the scan wait or raise prepare timeout.
- [low] Checkpoint budget is tight and the spec itself exceeds its target: 06 section 9 about 16.7 writes per submitted application for approval units alone; 07 section 9 adds 14,000 above budget; fleet about 66 against target 60, alert 80. Fix: Take the queueMatch direct-start lever (06 A-5) early; track durable.checkpoint_writes_per_submitted from P2.
- [medium] Rollout dependency chain is long: Autopilot needs P2 money, P3 verifier enforced 14 days, P5 hardening and P6 sensitive-answer encryption: 01 phase table P5; 04 3.1 step 3 (sensitive answers from profiles.sensitive_answers_enc, delivered in P6). Fix: Pull the sensitive-answer column forward into P3 or ship P3 without sensitive auto-fill (leave blank, force manual) and gate Autopilot on it.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Hostile employer page runs inside a process holding full app credentials, with no egress restriction: server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:21,56-60 (headless Chromium, bypassCSP, injected script, unrestricted network); applier boots with the same env as the api (server/src/lib/env.ts); Dockerfile.applier runs as pwuser but there is no network egress control (server/Dockerfile.applier:31-40). Fix: Implement the in-process egress guard and host allowlist (applications-59), a separate low-privilege DB role, and strip unneeded secrets from the applier env (applications-67).
- [medium] apply_url is trusted data fetched and navigated without re-validation in the applier: cloud-apply.ts:59 goto(row.applyUrl); URLs come from ATS adapters (server/src/services/ats/lever.ts:34, ashby.ts:49 use ATS-supplied applyUrl) with no host check at use time. Fix: Validate https scheme, no credentials and host in the five-host allowlist at precheck and again for each main-frame navigation.
- [low] Raw exception text stored as user-visible failure reason: server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:69 passes err.message into failureReason, shown by the web app. Fix: Map to coded messages (FAILURE_MESSAGES) and log the detail only.
- [medium] Truth lock bypass by direct API use on PATCH: answer and cover letter text are unchecked, lines checked by id only: server/src/routes/applications.ts:63-81 accepts arbitrary answers and coverLetter; lines pass only enforceTruthLock. Fix: Treat as the user's own words per spec, but run checkClaims and the verifier on model-originated text and mark source; keep drafts route as the only model path (already refuses sensitive labels, applications.ts:117).
- [low] Extension report is the only proof of a send and is unauthenticated beyond the session, with arbitrary receipt content: server/src/routes/extension.ts:31-34 and submissionReportSchema (shared/src/schemas.ts:62-69) accept any filledFields and confirmationText; a user can mark an approved application submitted without sending (self-charge only). Fix: Acceptable (harm is to the reporter) but mark extension receipts as client-asserted and keep the lease and duplicate alerts from the spec.
- [low] Rate limiting of approve, queue and report is the global in-memory 120 per minute per IP: server/src/middleware/rate-limit.ts:8-14; only /drafts has a per-user limiter (routes/resume.ts:26). Fix: Per-user limit classes (prepare 10/min, user_api 600/min) when the Postgres-backed limiter lands (P1).
- [low] Withdraw and report race can leave an employer-received application unrecorded: routes/applications.ts:139-148 withdraws approved items regardless of an open extension tab; later submitted report returns 409 (submissions.ts:71). Fix: Lease-aware withdraw and the late/terminal report rules of 08 3.3.

**Spec gaps**

- Frozen filler vs cover letters and resume lines: 04 section 3.1 (attachment plan 'the filler attaches the PDF or the letter') and the cover_letter column assume delivery, but extension/src/filler.ts:33-45 reads only answers and resumePdfUrl. Nothing in 04, 07 or 08 says how a cover letter or tailored lines reach the employer.
- PRD 7.6 changes the frozen filler (file name next to resumePdfUrl, D9), 07 3.2 item 9 injects filler.js 'byte for byte' and omits resumePdfUrl, and 07 acceptance criterion 1 forbids any change under extension/. The cloud name is also resume.pdf (07) versus '{Name} - Resume.pdf' (PRD).
- Render timing conflict: 04 renders the PDF inside prepare before draft_state ready; PRD 7.6 and call 15 freeze the snapshot at approval and render before send, failing the send clean with refund if rendering fails. resume_snapshot and resume_file_key (PRD 12) are absent from _db/applications.md and 02-database.
- PRD application_events (keyed by tracker_item_id, source, payload) conflicts with _db/applications.md 3.4 (keyed by application, kind, actor, detail, dedupe_key). The PRD says it governs until the re-architecture is approved, but worker 08 writes events inside settlement.
- PRD R4 and 05-api describe 'refunded' / 'credit refunded' for failed sends; the spec never sets refunded and never charges a failed send, so there is nothing to refund. UI copy and tracker badge wording need one rule.
- Starter Autopilot (01 B.6.3): approvals go to the extension queue but the frozen extension needs a human press, so 'hands-off' is only pre-approval. It reserves up to 35 credits a day against a 14-day return-to-draft window. Product meaning and credit effect are unstated.
- Extension failures in manual review mode are never reported (extension/src/background.ts:38-40), so the 'failed with no charge' rows in 08 3.3 only arise for auto-submit. The spec does not size the credit-reservation impact or the UX of 14 days reserved.
- Delayed enqueue and queue timeout clock are marked [verify] in 06 A-8 and 07 section 5; if DBOS starts timeoutMS at enqueue, a 10-hour Autopilot delay exceeds the 480 s timeout. The workaround (A-1 send_not_before plus reconciler) is stated only as a fallback.
- Sensitive-answer storage dependency: 04 reads profiles.sensitive_answers_enc in P3 and Autopilot guard 5 depends on it in P5, but encryption is a P6 deliverable; no interim behavior is specified.
- Spec 04 section 13.9 and 06 A-2: shared/src/plans.ts has dailyModelSpendCapUsd 0.5, 2, 5, 8 versus 0.05, 0.20, 0.25, 0.50 in 01 B.11.2 and 04-matching, and no autopilotDailySendCap field. Autopilot's 1.0x stop and the Starter 35 cap cannot be built until one set is chosen.
- Prepare worst case of about 660 s exceeds the 600 s workflow timeout (04 section 5.3); the spec accepts it and relies on reconcile-prepare marking prepare_timeout, which then discards a nearly finished draft.
- 04 loadAndPlan claims only draft_state pending, while 01 B.6.5 admits pending, failed and stale; 04 changes this without amending 01. Similarly the PATCH-on-pending semantics (04 13.1) and the PDF-clear reverify (13.2) are requests, not yet in 05-api 10.6 or 01 B.5.8.
- 08 D2 leaves the attempt row 'failed' with a non-ambiguous code while the application becomes failed/unconfirmed, so attempt and application disagree; analytics (submissions.needs_check) and A-SUB-7 can undercount.
- No spec says how the cloud applier handles ATS forms whose required questions are not in answers when form_questions source is unknown beyond the Autopilot skip (04 13.7); human-approved cloud sends can still go out and fail required_missing.
- The spec has no master README, so cross-references to INV-n, FR-n, A-DUR-n, S-AP1..4 and decisions D-n live only in 01 and cannot be traced from the worker specs.

**Open questions**

- Approve ADR-001 Revision 2 (postgres-durable, DBOS) now, or keep building the resume suite R1-R4 on today's Express and BullMQ first as PRD D7 says? The two plans both add columns to applications (resume_snapshot, draft_state) and both rewrite services/submissions.ts, which the PRD notes is owned by another session.
- Is the extension really frozen for the next release? A single filler change (file name, resumePdfUrl handling) and a client retry for reports would unlock PRD R2, safe late confirmation and cover-letter delivery. If it stays frozen, how should cover letters and resume lines reach employers (answer entries only, PDF only)?
- Should the cloud channel be switched off for real users until the egress guard, allowlist, kill switch and stuck-submitting sweeper exist? It is deployed today on sd1-applier.
- After a post-click error or crash, do you accept 'needs_check, no automatic resend, resend only with acknowledgement' as the default until spike S-AP2 proves submit interception? This trades some missed charges for no duplicate applications.
- What does Starter Autopilot mean given the frozen extension needs a human press, and should Starter be eligible at all?
- Which dailyModelSpendCapUsd set is correct (0.5/2/5/8 on disk or 0.05/0.20/0.25/0.50 in the spec), and are autopilotDailySendCap values 0/35/45/110 final?
- Credit language: a failed send is never charged. Should the UI and tracker say 'no credit used' instead of 'credit refunded'?
- Receipt data retention: keep full filled values (including EEO, visa, salary) for 7 days, 90 days or longer? Needs counsel for DPDP and GDPR before launch.
- Autopilot gating: confirm it opens only after 14 days of verifier enforcement with shadow false-accept at or below 1 percent, and who signs off the consent text and consent_version.
- Is a human-approved cloud send allowed for jobs with ghost_verdict possible or likely, or only the extension? Today neither is checked.
- Do cover letters need their own rendered PDF (Typst letter template) for ATS forms with a file field, or is plain text in a textarea enough for launch?
- Should unconfirmed (needs_check) sends ever be auto-confirmed by the forwarded confirmation email (reply classifier) to settle late confirmations, or only by a genuine extension report?

**Capacity**

## billing

Billing today is the phase-A skeleton: working Stripe checkout and portal, a signature-verified webhook that does store-and-process in one inline transaction, correct once-per-invoice grants (unique index), once-per-application charge, failed-never-charged, and cancel-subscriptions-before-account-delete. Almost everything the spec adds is missing: credit_balances cache and triggers, rollover/expiry (the old invoice-only unique index makes it impossible), store-then-process with retries/dead-letter, ordering guard, past-due 7-day grace (today past_due keeps the paid plan forever), zero-amount gate, price/line anomaly handling (today a paid invoice with no known price or user is marked processed with only a log warn), refund/dispute alerting, daily reconcile, 48-hour offer-pause with undo (today an offer-tagged email pauses Stripe immediately with no undo and no application link), idempotent customer/checkout creation, erasure via deletion_tasks, ledger archive, immutability, alerts and metrics. The most urgent defects are money-correctness ones in the live webhook: $0 invoices grant full credits, silent loss of unmapped paid events, no double-checkout guard, and no refund/dispute alert. Spec is internally mostly coherent but has unresolved contradictions (offer-pause routes P2 vs P6, 72h step retry vs round model, grace anchor, PRD wording 'credit refunded' vs failed-never-charged) and is silent on tax, currency, dunning emails, portal configuration and chargeback clawback.

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] Zero-amount invoices (100% promo codes) mint full credits: server/src/services/billing-events.ts:74-83 never checks amount_paid; checkout enables allow_promotion_codes server/src/routes/billing.ts:35. Fix: Add amount_paid gate now (S); restrict promotion codes to an allowlist or disable allow_promotion_codes until STRIPE_GRANT_COUPONS exists.
- [high/confirmed] Paid events silently lost: unknown price or missing metadata.userId is logged at warn and the event is recorded as processed: server/src/services/billing-events.ts:96-103; routes/webhooks.ts:39. Fix: Throw (roll back, let Stripe retry) or persist as dead with a page; add customer-id user fallback. Until the new pipeline lands, fail the request on unmapped price.
- [high/confirmed] No double-checkout guard: a paying user can open a second Checkout and get two live subscriptions (double charge, two grants): server/src/routes/billing.ts:26-41. Fix: Reject checkout (409, send to portal) when subscriptions.status in active/trialing/past_due with a live Stripe subscription; spec should state this.
- [high/confirmed] Refunds and disputes produce no alert and no credit action: billing-events.ts:93-103 ignores charge.* types; no handler. Fix: Add alert_only handling and page; decide clawback policy (owner).
- [medium] past_due keeps paid plan and cloud applier indefinitely: server/src/services/submissions.ts:9-13; billing-events.ts:10. Fix: Implement past_due_since + 7-day effectivePlan (or shorter) before charging real customers.
- [medium] No rollover cap: credits accumulate unbounded, undermining the 2x grant economics (FR-47): unique index invoice-only schema/billing.ts:27-29; no expiry code. Fix: Ship M2 index widening + rolloverExpiry with the balance lock in the same release as the cache table.
- [medium] Webhook processing holds a DB transaction open across a Stripe API call: server/src/routes/webhooks.ts:36-48 (ponytail comment); INLINE_REQUEST 10s. Fix: Acceptable at 270 events/day; a 60/min burst during a Stripe incident can pin ~10 pool connections for 10s each. Refetch before the transaction as an interim.
- [medium] Concurrent events for the same customer can apply an older refetched snapshot last (no ordering guard, no partitioned queue): routes/webhooks.ts:37-55; schema lacks stripe_event_created. Fix: Partitioned queue or per-customer advisory lock plus stripe_event_created guard.
- [medium] Account deletion leaves Stripe customer (with email) and loses the ledger audit trail: auth.ts:51-62 cancels subs only; ledger FK cascade schema/billing.ts:12-14. Fix: Implement cancelStripeForErasure + archive trigger before launch to paying users.
- [medium] Offer pause fires immediately with no undo/notice and on any 'offer' email, not only ones linked to a submitted application: server/src/jobs/classify-email.ts:34-40. Fix: Either gate behind a flag until the 48h flow exists or add the application-link and notice now; a false positive silently stops billing.
- [low] Pause does not stop applications: pause_collection voids invoices but credits keep being spent; applications_paused_at not implemented: classify-email.ts:34-40; no applications_paused_at in server/src. Fix: Intended behaviour must be confirmed; spec says approvals skip with reason paused.
- [medium] Sign-up bootstrap (profile, subscription row, free grant) is not atomic with user creation and not repaired: server/src/lib/auth.ts:94-111. Fix: Add the 5-minute reconciler and a one-free-grant unique index; saveSubscription is UPDATE-only so a missing row makes every subscription event a silent no-op (webhooks.ts:45-49).
- [medium] Deployment config: WEB_ORIGIN / BETTER_AUTH_URL unset on staging: task brief; checkout success/cancel/return URLs are built from WEB_ORIGIN (routes/billing.ts:36,37,47). Fix: Set both to the Vercel domain before any checkout test; also set STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET for the staging endpoint and register the 9 event types.
- [medium] Spec dependency: all P2 billing workflows assume DBOS durable seam which does not yet exist in server/: worker.ts BullMQ only. Fix: Per PRD D7 the resume suite ships on today's Express server; do the cheap fixes (amount gate, loud failures, double-checkout guard, alerting) on the current path and defer the workflow rebuild to P2.

**Security findings**

- [low] GET /api/me exposes Stripe customer and subscription ids and internal cost caps to the client: server/src/routes/me.ts:17-24 returns the raw subscriptions row and full getPlan() (dailyModelSpendCapUsd, interactiveSpendBackstopUsd). Fix: Serialize through an explicit pick-list per 05-api 12.4.
- [high/confirmed] Promotion-code abuse path via zero-amount grants: routes/billing.ts:35 + billing-events.ts:74-83. Fix: Amount gate and coupon allowlist (billing-33).
- [low] Webhook secret rotation not supported; STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET optional in production: env.ts:25-26; webhooks.ts:24-26. Fix: Refuse boot in production when STRIPE_SECRET_KEY set without webhook secret; add PREVIOUS secret.
- [low] No explicit signature tolerance and no body/content-type bound on the webhook: webhooks.ts:23,30. Fix: Pass tolerance 300 explicitly and set limit 1mb per 05-api 14.2.
- [low] Stripe calls on checkout/portal use SDK default 80s timeout and 2 retries (request-pinning, duplicate-create on retry without idempotency key): routes/billing.ts:21,29,45. Fix: Pass INLINE_REQUEST and idempotency keys.
- [low] Billing endpoints lack the dedicated 10/10min limiter (Stripe object creation by authenticated users): routes/index.ts:20,33. Fix: Add the 'billing' limiter class.
- [medium] No DB-level ledger immutability or role separation: any code path or SQL injection with the app role can rewrite credits: no REVOKE/trigger; single role. Fix: Ship credit_ledger_immutable trigger and role grants from _db/billing 2.3.
- [medium] Erasure incomplete for payment data: Stripe customer email retained: routes/billing.ts:21; no customers.del. Fix: billing-48; document Stripe as independent controller in the privacy notice.

**Spec gaps**

- Taxes are not specced anywhere: no Stripe Tax / automatic_tax, no tax-id or billing-address collection, no VAT/GST/sales-tax position. Matters before charging EU, UK, India or US customers.
- Currency is USD only (plans.ts priceUsd, R5 asserts usd); no spec on local pricing, FX or currency of refunds.
- Dunning and payment-failure communication is not specced: invoice.payment_failed only syncs state; no user email, no in-app banner, no Stripe smart-retry / email settings stated.
- Stripe Customer Portal configuration is not specced (which products/prices a user may switch to, proration behaviour, cancel-at-period-end vs immediate, pause allowed?). Proration safety depends on it.
- Mid-cycle upgrade grants no credits until the next renewal (proration-only invoice => proration_only, no grant). Spec never states whether the user should receive the delta credits immediately; likely a product bug for upgrades.
- No prevention of double checkout is specced (only detection in reconcile R2).
- Contradiction: 09 sec13 asks to move offer-pause undo routes to P2; 05-api 749-750 and 12.5 still say P6; 01 B.14 phase not updated. The other 15 requests in 09 sec13 (outcome_ck values proration_only/user_deleted, skip_reason no_longer_offer, lock position 1a, STRIPE_GRANT_COUPONS in 06 secrets, alerts A-BILL-4..7) are not merged into 01/02/05/06 yet.
- Contradiction: 01 B.6.11 says step retries 'up to 40 attempts over 72 hours'; 09 sec4.5 replaces it with a 7-round model of 8 attempts (needs 01 update, 09 item 5).
- Contradiction: 01 B.6.11 says grace is 7 days from current_period_end; _db/billing D8 anchors on past_due_since (Q-1 unresolved).
- Contradiction: B.6.11 says grant line is chosen by price lookup_key; 09 uses price id via STRIPE_PRICES; STRIPE_PRICES is JSON in env (single source of truth in Stripe lookup_keys vs env not reconciled with R5 price check).
- Contradiction on refunds: invariant and PRD say a failed send's 'credit is refunded' and status 'refunded' exists in shared/web; billing spec says failed is never charged and refund_failed_submit is operator-only. The 'refunded' application status is not defined in any billing/application state machine.
- Late-confirmation failed->submitted charging (_db/billing 4.2) conflicts with current recordSubmission rejecting non-approved states; needs the applications domain to agree.
- Chargeback/refund policy has no automated clawback (Q-4); no definition of what happens to granted credits already spent when a dispute is lost.
- Subscription cancellation semantics for users: cancel_at_period_end keeps credits until used? Credits of a canceled subscriber never expire (rollover only runs on a grant) - not stated.
- Free-plan rollover (Q-5) and 'Free grants once' vs plans.ts comment 'free plan: one-time grant' fine, but the 25 is hard-coded in auth.ts rather than getPlan('free').applicationsPerCycle; spec D-rule 'no plan numbers outside plans.ts' is violated in code.
- plans.ts lacks autopilotDailySendCap, dailyScoringCap, matchCadenceMinutes referenced by 05-api 12.1; dailyModelSpendCapUsd (0.5/2/5/8) disagrees with 01 and 04 (0.05/0.20/0.25/0.50) per 09 sec13 item 14.
- Erasure ordering: 09 puts Stripe cancel at worker erasure step X1 (after the user has been told 'deleting'), 01 B.6.11 keeps it in the Better Auth beforeDelete hook; 16-housekeeping owns the final answer but statement of who wins is only in 09 item 8.
- Webhook ingest assumes a request workflow with 5s wait; behaviour if the durable engine is down at cut-over (dual handling flag LIFECYCLE_V2) is specified only at a high level, and the flag name conflicts with _db/billing 'billing_store_then_process'.
- Spec has no policy for Stripe test-mode vs live data in staging beyond key prefix, and no spec for Stripe webhook endpoint registration (9 event types, API version) as code or checklist.

**Open questions**

- Tax: will Kairos use Stripe Tax and collect addresses/tax ids at launch? Which jurisdictions are you registered to sell in, and are prices tax-inclusive or exclusive?
- Mid-cycle upgrades: should an upgrade (e.g. Starter to Pro) grant the credit difference immediately, or only at the next renewal as the code and spec do?
- Past-due grace: confirm 7 days anchored on first past_due (not current_period_end), or should past_due drop to Free sooner?
- Chargebacks and refunds: do you want any automatic credit clawback or Autopilot/account hold on a lost dispute, or is page-the-owner enough for the beta?
- Promo codes: should customers be able to enter any promotion code at checkout? If yes, which coupons may grant credits on a $0 invoice (STRIPE_GRANT_COUPONS)?
- Refund policy for subscriptions: money-back window, prorated refunds on cancellation, and refunds on account deletion are not decided.
- The 'refunded' application status and 'credit refunded' copy: keep it (and define when it is set) or remove it since failed sends are never charged?
- Offer pause: confirm the 48-hour undo and the move of the undo routes to P2, and confirm that a paused user should also stop applying (applications_paused_at) rather than only stop being billed.
- Rollover: confirm the 2x grant cap applies to the whole balance including the 25 free credits (Q-5) and what happens to remaining credits when a subscriber cancels.
- Account deletion: is it acceptable to retain the Stripe customer when an invoice is open, and should accounts be deleted immediately or only after the current paid period?
- Retention of credit_ledger_archive and stripe invoice records: is a statutory period (e.g. 7 years) required for the owner's jurisdiction?
- Which Stripe account/mode is staging wired to, and is the webhook endpoint + signing secret registered for the Vercel/Railway URLs? (WEB_ORIGIN and BETTER_AUTH_URL are unset on sd1-api.)
- Pricing launch values (plans.ts says launch prices to be tested): are the 9 Stripe prices already created and does STRIPE_PRICES on Railway contain all of them?

**Capacity**

Balance/available credits are computed by SUM over credit_ledger plus a count over applications on every GET /api/me, /api/credits and approve. No queue, no reconcile.",
```json
  "assumptions": "10k users, ~2,500 paying, ~8,000 Stripe events/month (~270/day, 60/min burst), ~20k submitted applications/day, ~3,500 invoice grants/month; ledger ~7.5M rows/year (from _db/billing sec7).",

    "api: webhook p95 = Stripe refetch (~300ms, 10s cap) + tx; a 60/min burst x up to 10s holds 10 pooled connections in the worst Stripe-incident case; PgBouncer transaction mode in use.",
    "db: SUM(delta) per request is cheap for Free/Starter users (<~200 rows) but a heavy user reaches ~40k rows/year; credit_ledger_user_idx lacks INCLUDE(delta) so the sum is a heap scan; approve takes an advisory lock per user.",
    "stripe api: ~230 calls/day target, no concern; no queue-level rate protection if a reconcile is added.",
    "worker: nothing billing-related runs there today."
  ],
  "cliffs": [
    "Ledger SUM per read once heavy users exceed ~10k rows (hundreds of ms on /api/me).",
    "Stripe incident: inline refetch pins DB connections; no backpressure.",
    "Hash collisions of pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(userId)) serialize unrelated users (low impact).",
    "Single-process in-line webhook gives no durable retry beyond Stripe's 3 days; a Postgres outage > 3 days loses events with no reconcile to heal."
  ],
  "loadTestPlan": "Stripe test clocks: 50 synthetic customers across the 9 plan/interval pairs for a year of renewals; assert one grant per paid invoice and balance == SUM(delta). Replay each fixture event 10x concurrently and shuffled; 60 events/min for 10 min burst with a fake Stripe adding 300ms latency; k6 on /api/me and approve with a 40k-row ledger user.",
  "sloProposal": [
    "S7: 99.9% of Stripe events processed or ignored within 10 minutes of receipt.",
    "Zero duplicate grants and zero duplicate charges (hard invariant, page on any).",
    "Webhook endpoint p99 < 2s, availability 99.9% (so Stripe's retries are rare).",
    "Daily reconcile success in last 26 hours; missing_grants == 0 after each run.",
    "/api/me p95 < 300ms at 10k users with credit_balances cache."
  ],
  "realityCheck": "At 10k users the Stripe load is trivial; the real capacity risks are correctness ones (lost or mis-granted events, no heal path) and the per-read ledger SUM, not throughput. The current inline design is adequate for the beta; durable workflows and the cache table are about correctness and recoverability rather than scale."
}
```

## database

Today's database is the 17-table skeleton (0000) plus resumes and resume_revisions (0001) plus the llm_calls ledger family (0002): 23 tables, 9 enums, 3 migrations, one owner-level role used by api, worker and applier alike. Of the spec's 55 tables, 32 are missing; the 23 that exist are mostly thin (few CHECKs, no guard triggers, no encryption, no actor mechanism, no grants). Spec-critical infrastructure is absent: Postgres 18 (local and test are 17, Railway version unverified), ops/provision-roles.sql, role separation, PgBouncer-safe client settings, the custom migration runner (drizzle's migrate runs all pending files in one transaction with no lock), the expand-only linter, every retention job, the erasure workflow, backups/restore drill and Postgres in CI. The only ledger pieces built are the exactly-once charge index, the Stripe event id key, the partial unique on invoice, and the 0002 llm_calls partitioning with its SQL functions; those functions are never scheduled, and the seeded partitions run out on 2026-12-07. The resume-suite PRD (section 12) conflicts with 02-database.md on resumes (many bases per profile vs one per slot), application_events (tracker-keyed vs application-keyed), revision reasons and retention, and migration numbering (PRD says 0003 and up on today's server; the spec reserves 0003 to 0033); none of the PRD tables exist. Biggest correctness risks: parse-resume deletes confirmed facts on re-parse, the applier runs a browser with full DB credentials, and CI never exercises Postgres.

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] llm_calls partitions run out around 2026-12-07 with nothing scheduled to create more: 0002 seeds current week plus 6 (0002_platform_02_llm_ledger.sql:235); journal when=1792851220097 (about 2026-10-24); no caller of llm_calls_ensure_partitions anywhere in server/src; worker.ts:27 is the only scheduler. Fix: Add a daily scheduled job now (BullMQ repeatable until DBOS lands) calling ensure_partitions, rollup and drop_old; alert if llm_calls_default holds a row.
- [high/confirmed] Spec DDL needs Postgres 18 but local, test and CI-adjacent images are 17 and Railway's version is unverified: docker-compose.yml:6; server/test/helpers/test-db.ts:13; 02-database.md R-6 (uuidv7 defaults), S-P1 not run. Fix: Run S-P1 first. Until PG18 is confirmed, write new DDL with gen_random_uuid() and add uuidv7 defaults in a later migration.
- [high/confirmed] Current migration runner cannot execute the first planned P1 migrations: server/src/migrate.ts:6 drizzle migrate runs all pending files in one transaction; 0006 needs ALTER TYPE ADD VALUE and 0014 needs CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY. Fix: Build the per-file runner with advisory lock, no-transaction headers and lock_timeout before any further schema migration.
- [high/confirmed] CI never touches Postgres, so schema, constraints and migrations are unvalidated on every PR: .github/workflows/ci.yml has no postgres service or TEST_DATABASE_URL; integration tests gated at server/test/helpers/test-db.ts:16. Fix: Add a postgres:18 service to CI, run migrate-from-0000 plus *.int.test.ts, add drizzle-kit check and the FK-index query.
- [high/confirmed] PRD section 12 and 02-database.md describe incompatible resume, event and revision schemas; building either first forces rework of the other: 02-database.md 7.1.7 (resumes_user_slot_uq, one per slot), 7.3.4 (application_events.application_id NOT NULL), 7.1.8 (reason set); resume-suite/README.md:366-374. Fix: Decide the owner of these tables, then amend 02-database.md before writing R1 migrations; PRD already says it will be reconciled.
- [medium] Role split is a large cutover on a live staging database (ownership transfer, REVOKE on public, new credentials in three Railway services): 02-database.md 4.1; env.ts:10 single DATABASE_URL. Fix: Stage it: provision roles, move processes one at a time (applier first), keep the old owner role until T-DB-1 passes.
- [medium] Trigger and guard volume (30+ triggers, three status-guard versions, soak-gated contracts) is the dominant schedule risk of the domain: 02-database.md 8.2, 14.3 gates (14-day soaks for 0025/0026). Fix: Sequence guards by invariant value: facts_guard, credit ledger immutability and the status guard first; defer the rest.
- [medium] Multiple FKs have no leading-column index, so cascades and erasure scan whole tables: server/src/db/schema/profile.ts:52,82; applications.ts:24-26,53; jobs.ts:62. Fix: Add the indexes in the owning domain migration and enforce with the 11.4 query.
- [medium] Disk plan: spec's own ceiling is 108 GB against a 150 GB growth volume (72% in year one): 02-database.md 12.3, Q-DB-2. Fix: Alert at 70%, size volume to 250 GB before beta ramp; measure real row sizes in P8.
- [medium] Migration sequence and backfills (14.3, 14.4) are specified but never run against real data: 02-database.md:9 (only the 245 section-7/8.3 statements were executed). Fix: Rehearse T-DB-4 on a restored staging dump before each release.
- [medium] Session-state hazards behind PgBouncer: DBOS LISTEN needs direct connections, migrations and advisory locks need unpooled URL: 02-database.md 4.4; migrate.ts:6 uses pooled client. Fix: Verify in the Railway half of S-D1; add the unpooled URL before the runner rewrite.
- [low] Drift between shared/ zod enums and DB checks (below_bar is server-only; closed sets now text+CHECK): 02-database.md 14.1 (MATCH_STATUSES_DB); server/src/db/schema/enums.ts:2-22. Fix: One contract test comparing shared constants to pg_enum and CHECK definitions.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Applier process (headless browser on third-party pages) holds full read/write database credentials: server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:31-69 uses db(); server/src/applier.ts imports closeDb; env.ts:10 one DATABASE_URL. Fix: Give the applier its own role limited to scan_target_v and the seven functions (02-database 4.3) before widening cloud apply.
- [high/confirmed] No privilege separation: every process uses one owner-level role; ledger, events and flags are writable and deletable by any code path: no GRANT/REVOKE in server/drizzle/*.sql; client.ts:13. Fix: Provision roles and the grant matrix; add immutability triggers on credit_ledger and application_events.
- [high/confirmed] Re-parse deletes every fact for the profile including confirmed facts: server/src/jobs/parse-resume.ts:14. Fix: Replace only unconfirmed facts of the same import; move to facts FK SET NULL and a guard that blocks deleting cited confirmed facts.
- [medium] Truth-lock and approve-before-send have no database backstop: any writer can set facts.confirmed or move an application to submitting/submitted: schema has no triggers or CHECKs (server/src/db/schema/profile.ts:75-90, applications.ts:15-43). Fix: Ship facts_guard with kairos.actor, applications_approval_shape_ck and status guard v1.
- [medium] Personal content stored in plaintext: resume source text and reply bodies; no encryption envelope or key list exists: server/src/db/schema/profile.ts:34; applications.ts:57; env.ts lacks DATA_KEKS. Fix: Ship lib/crypto.ts, source_text_enc and body encryption with dual write, then drop plaintext columns.
- [medium] Sensitive answers (EEO, visa, salary) persist in applications.answers with no scrub or separate encrypted store: server/src/db/schema/applications.ts:30; no scrub job. Fix: Add profiles.sensitive_answers_enc and the 7-day scrub-applications job (INV-9).
- [medium] Auth rate limiting is per-process memory, so brute force scales with replica count: server/src/lib/auth.ts:69 (rateLimit without database storage). Fix: Create auth_rate_limit and set rateLimit.storage = database.
- [medium] Free credit grant is not constrained to one per user and is issued by a non-atomic post-create hook: server/src/lib/auth.ts:94-111; billing.ts:22-30 (no free_grant unique). Fix: Add credit_ledger_one_free_grant_uq and make profile/subscription/grant creation reconcilable.
- [medium] Account erasure is a bare cascade: no tombstone, no ledger archive, llm_calls user_hash and contact_messages not scrubbed, financial rows destroyed: server/src/lib/auth.ts:48-63; billing.ts:14,55-62. Fix: Implement the 16.2 workflow with deletion_tasks and archive_user_ledger.
- [medium] Migration runner is unserialized: concurrent deploys can race on DDL and a failure leaves one large transaction holding locks: server/src/migrate.ts:6. Fix: Advisory lock, lock_timeout, per-file transactions on an unpooled migrator connection.
- [low] Default development credentials hardcoded as drizzle-kit fallback: server/drizzle.config.ts:7. Fix: Fail when DATABASE_URL is unset for non-local use.

**Spec gaps**

- PRD section 12 vs 02-database 7.1.7: PRD allows many base resumes per profile plus tailored copies; spec has one resume per slot (UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (user_id, resume_profile_id)) and defers multi-row to phase 2 (0033). The PRD's per-profile default partial unique also cannot constrain the NULL-profile resume.
- application_events: spec keys it to applications (application_id NOT NULL, 16 fixed kinds, scalar-only detail <= 1 KB); PRD keys it to tracker items with a free payload jsonb, and tracker-only items have no application at all.
- Migration numbering: PRD says R1-R4 migrations are 0003 and up on today's server; the spec fixes 0003 = platform_00_dbos_datasource through 0033. Neither document says who renumbers, and the Drizzle journal is linear.
- resume_revisions: spec reasons (save, pre_ai, pre_autopilot, restore, import, fact_change) and retention (100 newest + named + referenced) differ from PRD (edit, named, before_ai, before_tailor, restore; 50 unnamed + all named). resume_revisions_name_uq (resume_id, lower(name)) would reject repeated automatic named revisions such as before_tailor.
- facts_import_identity_uq (resume_profile_id, source_span_hash) and employments_import_uq cannot hold several imports per profile (PRD D8); they need import_id in the key.
- PRD adds applications.resume_file_key but 02-database already has resume_pdf_key; resume_snapshot has no size bound, retention, scrub rule or row_version/immutability treatment.
- PRD 9.3 requires a job-description snapshot that survives posting removal, but tracker_items lists no snapshot column and the jobs retention rules (180-day purge/delete) would remove the source.
- PRD tables lack: column lists for contacts, contact_jobs and interviews; tracker_items unique (user_id, job_id) and (user_id, application_id); stage CHECK set; fractional position type; per-user caps; FK delete rules (tracker_items.job_id, resumes.job_id); size bounds; grants; retention; erasure inventory; the PRD's own mapping of 'Drafting/Applied' to applications.status and application_events.stage.
- 02-database section 1 says 19 tables exist and 36 are new, but 0002 already built 4 of the 36 (23 exist, 32 remain). 0002's header says grants ship with ops/provision-roles.sql while V2-2 and 14.3 put them in 0004.
- Driver contradiction: spec says pg@8 and the node-postgres migrator; code, tests and the declared-stable test helper use postgres.js, and no migration step or owner is given for the swap.
- pg_stat_statements is preloaded in 13.1 but no migration or provisioning step creates the extension; DATABASE_UNPOOLED_URL and DATABASE_BACKUP_URL are named but absent from env.ts and 06 naming is not cross-checked.
- Account table comment allows only 'credential' or 'google', but the UI shows Apple, GitHub and LinkedIn; no data-model decision (provider ids, email-relay handling, scope rules) exists for them.
- ops/provision-roles.sql and ops/provision-dbos.sql are required before the first migration yet are neither written nor placed in the phase plan; the live-DB ownership transfer procedure is one sentence.
- No master README.md for the spec set, so migration, phase and flag names are cross-referenced by hand across 01, 02, 05 and 06.

**Open questions**

- Is ADR-001 (Postgres 18 + DBOS) approved? Until it is, should the PRD tables (R1-R4) be written on Postgres 17-compatible DDL (gen_random_uuid, no custom runner) and fold into 02-database later?
- Which document wins for resumes, application_events and revisions: amend 02-database to the PRD shapes now, or keep the spec and change the PRD?
- What Postgres major version and plan does Railway kairos-sd1-staging run, does it support the postgres-ssl:18 image, start parameters, PITR and a per-database PgBouncer, and which URL does sd1-api use today (pooled or direct)?
- Is LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic enabled on staging now? If so, who runs partition maintenance before DBOS exists, given the runway ends about 2026-12-07?
- Keep postgres.js or switch to pg@8 for the whole server now (needed for the DBOS datasource and the spec's migrator)?
- Which social providers (Apple, GitHub, LinkedIn) are in scope for launch, since each changes the account table and the sign-in rules?
- Does staging hold real user data? That decides whether the owner/role cutover and ownership transfer can be done destructively.
- Retention and legal choices: credit_ledger_archive indefinitely (Q-DB-8), application_events until 25 GB (Q-DB-7), 90-day llm_calls, DPDP nomination and grievance SLA (Q-DB-12) all need counsel or owner sign-off.
- Should tracker items keep a permanent copy of the job description, and for how long, given PRD 9.3 versus the 180-day jobs purge?
- Is encrypting resume sources before the paid beta (Q-DB-1 default: dual-write from P3) acceptable, or must it ship in P1?

**Capacity**

Today: one Postgres, one pool of 10 per process (api, worker, applier), no PgBouncer use confirmed, BullMQ on Redis for queues.",

Credit balance and in-flight are COUNT/SUM queries per request (credits.ts:16-30) on an index (user_id, created_at); applications and matches have only two indexes each; no retention so tables only grow. Connection use is roughly 10 per process times replicas against Railway's default limit of about 100 (as-built S-08).",

```json
    "llm_calls inserts fall into llm_calls_default after about 2026-12-07",
    "Unindexed FK cascades (matches.job_id, applications.job_id) make job deletion and account erasure scan tables once rows reach the hundreds of thousands",

    "jobs growth (no purge) toward the 4.6 million rows of the growth case without trigram or visible_at indexes"

    "Free/Starter: negligible database load; one profile, one resume, few matches",
    "Pro/Max: cloud apply adds attempt writes and heartbeats on applications until submission_attempts exists; fillfactor and HOT discipline not applied yet",
    "Platform: llm_calls about 260,000 rows a day at growth, weekly partitions handle it once maintained"

  "realityCheck": "The spec's numbers are estimates from one scratch-container validation, not measurements. The 108 GB ceiling and 13 DBOS writes a second are modelled; nothing in the repo measures them yet.",

    "p99 api write transaction under 150 ms excluding Stripe/LLM waits",
```

## llm-agents

The P1 gateway core exists and is unit-tested against a fake client: strict-schema generation with committed snapshots, cache-layout request builder, retry/repair/tier-up ladder, circuit breaker, limiter, price table, fence, redaction functions and 6 of 12 agents at v1 with a prompt lock. It is not live. LLM_GATEWAY defaults to "legacy" (server/src/lib/env.ts:48), no service caller passes userId/plan/jobId (so per-user budgets, allowance and metadata.user_id never apply), the ops-flags, llm_cost_daily roll-up and partition jobs that the budget code reads do not exist, and NO live Anthropic call has ever been made (phase-A plan). Everything beyond the gateway core is missing: the 6 other agents (jd-analyst, claim-verifier, claim-verifier-escalation, reply-classifier, outcome-analyst, interview-coach), checkClaims entity containment, the independent verifier, the F11 allowance, the whole eval harness (server/evals does not exist, zero golden sets), telemetry, reconciliation, drift, batch path, canary/shadow/model pins, and PRD R3 writer changes. Redaction and the sensitive-line filter are built or specced but not applied to resume text, so contact data and visa/salary lines reach the model today. Two spec conflicts need an owner call: F11 status 402 (PRD, workspace README) vs 429 (ADR/03), and plans.ts dailyModelSpendCapUsd (0.5 to 8) vs the cost model (0.03 to 0.16).

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] Gateway is dark and unverified against the live API: env.ts:48 default legacy; phase-A plan: 'No live Anthropic call has run yet'; thinking/effort/enum-in-schema values are [V]/[verify] only. Fix: Run S-A1 on the evals key, then set LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic on staging with a user ctx; a 400 on any snapshot would fail every call of that agent.
- [high/confirmed] Budget enforcement is effectively off in production shape: No caller passes userId (score-matches.ts:60, resume.ts:134); llm_cost_daily never populated (no caller of llm_cost_rollup_day); shedLevel never set (spend-store.ts:73); ops_flags absent. Fix: Wire ctx in all six call sites, schedule the roll-up every minute and partition creation weekly, add ops_flags.
- [high/confirmed] No F11 allowance on user-triggered model routes: routes/resume.ts:26-28 writerLimiter 10 per minute only; routes/applications.ts:100, routes/jobs.ts:114. Fix: Ship allowance.ts with refund-on-failure before opening the workspace to Free users; a Sonnet suggestion call costs up to $0.05.
- [high/confirmed] No independent verifier and thin deterministic claims check: No claim-verifier agent; invented-details.ts:11-17 checks numbers and skills only. Fix: Add employer/title/school/URL/email containment (checkClaims) first, then the verifier in shadow; do not enable Autopilot or show drafted cover letters as 'verified' before that.
- [high/confirmed] Zero evals and zero golden sets: server/evals absent; evalSet paths dangle (fit-scorer/spec.ts:35). Fix: Build runner plus deterministic graders and the injection/sensitive/adversarial subsets before any v2 prompt; PRD R3 acceptance requires an eval at every level.
- [medium] budget_stopped rows violate the llm_calls CHECK constraint: gateway.ts:148-161 errorCode null vs drizzle/0002_platform_02_llm_ledger.sql:48. Fix: Write a code such as budget_<scope>; add a Testcontainers ledger test. Today each refusal wastes ~2.1 s of retries and loses the row.
- [medium] Limiter AIMD resets the bucket to a full burst on 429: llm/limiter.ts:117-121; reducedUntil never read. Fix: Apply a reduced refill rate while reducedUntil is in the future; make acquire wait up to maxWaitMs for a token.
- [medium] Tier-up on budget and auth failures spends more instead of failing: gateway.ts:132 (no error-code filter). Fix: Allow tier-up only for schema_invalid, max_tokens, refusal, circuit_open and exhausted overload retries.
- [medium] Deferred model work is dropped by the BullMQ jobs: jobs/score-matches.ts:67-69 swallows errors; retryable flag unused by jobs. Fix: Map retryable AgentError to a BullMQ delayed retry or a pending marker until DBOS arrives.
- [medium] Gateway cover-letter path lost length caps: draft-writer.ts:100-105 vs 113-123; schema max 12 paragraphs. Fix: Reapply 4 paragraphs and 250 words after verification in the shared tail, or add them to postValidate.
- [medium] Resume text silently truncated at about 9,000 characters on the gateway path: agents/resume-parser/render.ts:9 (maxTokens 3,000) vs schema 30,000 and legacy 20,000 (services/resume-parser.ts:33). Fix: Window long resumes (parser v2) or fail loudly; users otherwise confirm an incomplete fact set.
- [medium] Anthropic tier spend cap below projected spend: 03 s6.2: $1,000/month Build cap vs ~$4,092 growth spend. Fix: Apply for Scale or Custom before beta passes ~2,500 users; keep workspace limits under the cap.
- [medium] Cost model and plan caps disagree: cost-model.md s17.2 dailyModelSpendCapUsd 0.03-0.16 vs shared/src/plans.ts:69,94,119 (0.5, 2, 5); cost-model.md still names OpenRouter models. Fix: Mark cost-model.md section 17 superseded and let the owner pin the per-user numbers.
- [low] Same-family verifier and judge: 03 s13.3, 01 B.9.6 (decision A: Claude only). Fix: Accept per spec but keep every truth/safety gate deterministic and the 200-case self-preference set.
- [low] Large-fact users can trip the per-call cost cap: estimate.ts worst case vs answer-drafter maxCostPerCallUsd 0.0012; no 60-fact cap. Fix: Implement retrieval caps (B.9.7) before enabling the gateway for power users.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Sensitive personal data (visa, work authorization, DOB, salary, government ids) reaches the model and the fact bank: services/resume-parser.ts:19-23 and agents/resume-parser/render.ts:9 send the whole resume; facts then feed fit-scorer (jobs/score-matches.ts:56-60) and drafters. Fix: Implement dropSensitiveLines and the V7 output scan before any further parser use; INV-9 says sensitive items never enter a prompt.
- [high/confirmed] Contact details (name, email, phone, links) sent to Anthropic unredacted: redactContact/redactResumeHeader have no call sites (llm/redact.ts; grep); resume-parser.ts:21 comment. Fix: Call redactResumeHeader in the parse job with the profile, redactContact on fact text before scorer/drafter prompts; ZDR addendum is still unsigned.
- [high/confirmed] Cost-abuse exposure: authenticated users can drive Sonnet/Haiku spend with only a per-minute limiter: routes/resume.ts:26-28 limit 10/min; no allowance, no user budget ctx. Fix: Allowance plus per-user ctx before enabling the gateway; keep global envelope and org console limit as backstops.
- [high/confirmed] Employer-bound text relies on prompt-only truth for employers, titles, schools and scope: services/invented-details.ts:6-17; no claim-verifier. Fix: Add entity containment and the verifier; an injected job description could steer a cover letter to claim an invented employer without detection.
- [medium] Cached user block is unfenced when no userId is supplied: agents/*/render.ts userBlock branches; no caller sets ctx.userId. Fix: Make fence mandatory (fall back to a per-call nonce) and fail the render test if text is unfenced.
- [medium] Legacy path logs provider/validation error text and raw error objects: services/llm.ts:88,119; jobs/score-matches.ts:68; REDACT_PATHS lib/logger.ts:10-20 lacks content paths. Fix: Log codes only; add pino redaction paths from 03 s10.4; remove the legacy path.
- [low] Batch and interactive workspaces share a key via ANTHROPIC_API_KEY fallback: llm/client.ts:48. Fix: Remove the fallback once LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic is default; require per-workspace keys.
- [low] Enum-case normalizer mutates free-text strings and echo canary lacks source exemption: llm/schema.ts:26-39; llm/fence.ts:61-87. Fix: Normalize only at enum paths; implement the parser spec's source-line exemption (never for nonce or markers).
- [low] Reply and ops side channels not defended: no llm_paused automation on spend_limit/auth errors: errors.ts classifies but nothing acts. Fix: Build the error-class side effects (see llm-agents-16).

**Spec gaps**

- F11 refusal status is contradictory: resume-suite PRD s11 and resume-workspace README line 100 say 402; ADR-001 (01 B.8.10, Q7) and 03 s9.3 say 429 rate_limited with details.reason daily_allowance. The PRD is owner-approved 2026-10-29 and 01 is still Proposed.
- Only resume-parser has an agent spec. For the other 11 agents there are no complete prompts, strict schemas, postValidate rules, eval case formats beyond counts, or rubrics (03 s3.3 gives only a configuration row). jd-analyst needs the JobCard schema (01 B.7.7) and the verifier needs its claim/verdict wire format.
- Spec versions disagree on shapes: 01 B.9.2 resume-parser output (employments with offsets) vs agents/01-resume-parser.md (line ranges, [L n] tags); answer-drafter answers[] with abstain vs code's single answer; resume-writer 'before' field; outcome-analyst batch 'Always' (01 B.9.11) vs 'never' (03 s3.3, s18).
- cost-model.md is stale (OpenRouter models, per-user caps 0.03-0.16, envelope sum $82); 03 s0 says token counts still apply but the dollar caps conflict with plans.ts and 03 s9.1.
- 03 assumes DBOS steps (defer = step retry, DBOS checkpoints carry only ids) and modules/ paths; the PRD decision D7 builds R1-R4 on today's Express/BullMQ with services/. There is no interim contract for how a retryable AgentError is consumed in BullMQ.
- Echo canary (03 s11.4) flags 'assistant:', 'user:', 'as an ai', 'system prompt' in any output string; the parser spec requests a source-line exemption (V8, s13) that 03 does not adopt. Legitimate resume text will trip it and bypass repair.
- 03 s9.4 says interactive agents continue past 100% of their envelope, but s9.3 lists 'agent' as a refusal scope with 429 service_budget; the intended behavior for an interactive agent at 100% is ambiguous.
- 03 s17 puts the verifier shadow in P3 but defines no shadow comparison storage (where shadow verdicts are persisted, how disagreement is measured) beyond llm_calls call_kind='shadow'.
- llm_calls_model_ck hard-codes claude-(haiku|sonnet)-5-5 ids; 03 s14 model upgrade (dated snapshot ids, new model) has no migration step.
- The spec requires 100 owner-labelled judge-calibration cases per drafting agent but no labelling tool, format location (human-labels.jsonl is named, process is not) or schedule exists; P3 exit depends on it.
- No spec for how the web should render 503 ai_unavailable / 422 ai_refused / 429 service_budget (03 Q5 asks the owner; the PRD API tables list only 429/402).

**Open questions**

- F11: 402 (PRD, workspace README, freehire) or 429 (ADR-001, 03)? Frozen clients read 402 as 'no credits'; which should a spent daily AI allowance use?
- Is LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic (and ANTHROPIC_KEY_INTERACTIVE/BATCH, USER_HASH_KEY) set on Railway sd1-api and sd1-worker, or is staging still on the legacy chatJson path? Is there a live Anthropic key and which tier, and may S-A1 spend real money on it?
- Anthropic tier: will you apply for Scale/Custom before the paid beta passes ~2,500 users, and sign the ZDR addendum before beta?
- Per-user daily model spend caps: plans.ts 0.5/2/5/8 USD or the cost model's 0.03/0.06/0.10/0.16? (03 marks the plans.ts numbers [est] until you pin them.)
- Do you accept Opus 5.5 as judge (same family) and will you label 100 cases per drafting agent (about 6 hours) before P3 exit?
- Reconciliation: weekly from your machine, or a full Admin key in the worker with quarterly rotation?
- Shed thresholds $165 warn / $190 / $220 / $260: confirm.
- Sequencing: should PRD R3 (resume-writer@v2, headline, tags) ship on the current gateway with only deterministic checks, or wait for the eval harness and verifier? R3 acceptance says invented-detail rate 0 at every level, which needs evals.
- Parser: ship a quick fix now (redactResumeHeader plus a sensitive-line filter on v1) or wait for the full v2 line-range redesign (XL) in P3?
- Should the legacy chatJson path be deleted as soon as the gateway is live on staging, rather than waiting for P3?

## matching

Matching is about 15% built, and the built part is the P0/phase-A skeleton. What exists: Greenhouse/Lever/Ashby adapters, a BullMQ minute poller, the unchanged hard-filter gate (passesHardFilters), the deterministic F1 display score on the workspace fit route, a paste-a-URL importer, and the frozen GET /api/matches. What is missing is nearly all of the P4 design. There is no server/src/modules/, no DBOS and no durable/ directory. The jobs/companies/matches schema has none of the new columns. The spec's whole catalog tail is also absent: normalizePosting, families, ETag polling, ghost signal, SQL prefilter, candidateTick/candidatePage, user_match_state, pacing, backfill, rescore, jd-analyst cards, F1 pre-rank, scoreUser, below_bar and tombstones. The production-relevant problem is that the live scoring path is the uncapped per-posting loop the spec replaces. score-matches loops every user with preferences for every new job (score-matches.ts:30-70). It has no daily scoring cap, no spend cap, no eligibility check and no F1 pre-rank. It calls scoreFit with serviceCtx({}) and no userId (fit.ts:95), so the gateway's per-user budget scope is skipped (budget.ts:118). The P1 'interim controls on the old loop' that B.14 requires before a paid beta were never built (the phase A plan does not list them). Preference saves trigger no backfill (profile.ts:40-47), so a new user's feed stays empty until new postings arrive (D-20). The most serious security issue is that apply_url is taken unvalidated from board payloads and navigated by the cloud applier (lever.ts:34, ashby.ts:49, cloud-apply.ts:59). Any user can also add an arbitrary board to the polled set through the import route (routes/jobs.ts:29). The spec is mostly buildable but has real contradictions, listed under specGaps. Highest-value next steps: (1) interim caps and eligibility on the old loop plus plans.ts fields (days); (2) apply_url host allowlist and import hardening; (3) then the P4 build in the order migrations, normalizePosting, DBOS poll, prefilter and gate, cards, F1 pre-rank, pacing, scoreUser, backfill.
