**Risks**

- [critical/confirmed] Live scoring loop is uncapped and unmetered: score-matches.ts:30-70 (every user with preferences, every new job, no cap, no F1 floor, no eligibility); fit.ts:95 serviceCtx({}) so budget.ts:118 per-user scope never applies; queues.ts:40-44 attempts:3 re-runs all users (S-02); poll-boards.ts:20-21 first poll of a company enqueues every posting. My estimate at spec volumes is about 256,500 gate passes a day at base, so about $54 a day at the scorer's $0.00021 against the designed $24 (up to about 3x on retries). That is about 1M a day at growth. The legacy LLM path's price is not covered by this figure.. Fix: Before any paid beta, ship the P1 interim control on the old loop: dailyScoringCap and spend counters in user_daily_usage checked before each scoreFit call, eligibility (credits, billing pause, 7-day activity), pass userId in the call context, a pause_scoring kill switch, and a spy test. Add a company-level burst limit for first polls.
- [high/confirmed] O(users x new jobs) in-process fan-out will not scale to 10,000 users: score-matches.ts:30 loads all profiles; :37-47 does one count query per user per job; serial for-loop. About 10k users x 2 ms is about 20 s per job before any model call; 12,000 jobs a day at growth is about 67 worker-hours a day on 4 slots (worker.ts:15); a long job risks BullMQ stalled-job redelivery and double spend.. Fix: Do the P4 prefilter plus candidateTick (matching-43, matching-63). In the interim, short-circuit with a single SQL prefilter query per job (roles/location/salary) and page the users.
- [high/confirmed] No backfill on preference save or onboarding (D-20): routes/profile.ts:40-47; grep finds no backfill code. A new or edited-preferences user receives matches only from postings polled after the save.. Fix: Ship a minimal backfill (SQL prefilter on open jobs, capped K) triggered by the preferences PUT and first confirmed resume, ahead of the full P4 pipeline.
- [high/confirmed] A truncated or partial board listing mass-closes jobs; closed jobs can never reopen: poll-boards.ts:39-47 guards only the empty case; poll-boards.ts:59 onConflictDoNothing means closed_at is never cleared (D-19). Fix: Add the shrink guard and two-strike confirmation (matching-13), the guarded upsert with reopen (matching-14) and a closed_by column; the cheap version is a 50% shrink check in closeMissingJobs plus clearing closed_at on conflict.
- [high/confirmed] Pasted boards join the polled catalog and fan out to every user: routes/jobs.ts:23-37 inserts active:true; rate limit is in-memory 10 a minute (routes/jobs.ts:72-79); the poller then enqueues score-matches for all jobs of the new board for all users.. Fix: Create imported companies inactive (spec) and gate activation behind the owner or a quota; apply the jobImports allowance and a DB-backed limit.
- [high/confirmed] Cost model invalidated by plans.ts spend caps: plans.ts:69,93,118,144 set dailyModelSpendCapUsd 0.5/2/5/8 versus spec 0.05/0.20/0.25/0.50 (04 §8.1; 01 B.7.4). That is 10x to 16x, and the per-user pipeline cap is the only per-user ceiling once the gateway is on. plans.ts has no dailyScoringCap.. Fix: Owner to pin the numbers. If drafting shares the pipeline budget, split scoring and drafting caps. Add dailyScoringCap now.
- [medium] Priority polling never activates: Nothing writes companies.priority (grep); plans.ts:129 priorityPolling is unused. The Max '5-minute polling' value proposition is unmet; the express lane would never fire.. Fix: Build refresh-priority (matching-07) or, as a stopgap, set priority from active Max users' target companies.
- [medium] Two-character role names bypass the role filter: fit.ts:29-38 drops words of 2 characters or fewer and Array.every on [] is true; preferencesSchema allows roles of 2+ characters (shared schemas.ts:6). A role such as 'QA', 'PM' or 'UX' matches every title, which inflates scoring spend. The spec pins this behavior (04 §6.2 anyRoleMatchesAll).. Fix: Owner decision. Better: require at least one word of 3 or more characters, or apply a stricter match for short roles, and update the property test with it.
- [medium] Imported matches: NULL score sorts first and failed fits are stored as final 'skipped': routes/jobs.ts:59-68 inserts status new with no score; matches.ts:29 orderBy(desc(score)) puts NULLs first in Postgres. Failed-gate imports are written skipped (jobs.ts:62), which becomes an immutable user decision under R-18 and pollutes the queue-rate metric.. Fix: Score on import (or order NULLS LAST), and use below_bar for gate-failed imports once the enum value exists.
- [medium] Poll telemetry is invisible: Only logger.info (poll-boards.ts:86) and a free-text last_poll_error (poll-boards.ts:72). An adapter schema change (zod throw) shows up only as retries.. Fix: Add the minimum metrics (outcome counts, oldest due age, rejected postings) and A-ING-1/3/4 before cutting traffic over.
- [medium] Company names unreliable for the exclude-company gate: fit.ts:60-61 compares lowercased companies.name; imports derive names from the board token (job-import.ts:29) and the poller never refreshes names.. Fix: Resolve names from the ATS payload where available and match excluded companies on name or board token.
- [medium] Reposts, ghost postings and duplicate roles reach users: No family dedup (matching-21) and no ghost signal (matching-33 to matching-37); Ashby unlisted postings are dropped rather than flagged (ashby.ts:37).. Fix: Sequence F2 structural evidence with P4 and gate Autopilot on it before enabling Autopilot.
- [low] Lever EU boards cannot be polled: lever.ts:47 hard-codes api.lever.co, so an EU board returns 404 and the company is deactivated by poll-boards.ts:72.. Fix: Add api_region to companies and the EU host to the adapter.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Unvalidated apply_url from third-party boards is navigated by the cloud applier: lever.ts:34 (p.applyUrl) and ashby.ts:49 (j.applyUrl) take the URL from the board payload; no host check on insert (poll-boards.ts:55-60, jobUpsertValues job-import.ts:38-51); cloud-apply.ts:59 `page.goto(row.applyUrl)`. Any user can create a free Lever or Ashby board and paste its job link; the import creates an active polled company (routes/jobs.ts:29) and the user's own applier then visits an attacker-chosen host (internal Railway addresses, metadata endpoints, attacker pages).. Fix: Enforce the jobs_apply_url_host_ck allowlist (the 5 ATS apply hosts) in the adapter, in the DB and in the applier precheck; reject postings that fail it. Land the applier egress guard (06 §7.4) before enabling the cloud applier for imported jobs.
- [high/confirmed] Any signed-in user can add arbitrary boards to the polled, all-user-scored catalog: routes/jobs.ts:23-37 (active:true, onConflictDoNothing on (ats, boardToken)); limiter is per-process memory (routes/jobs.ts:72-79); no ai_usage jobImports accounting. Result: unbounded poll load and, via poll-boards.ts:66, LLM spend for every user on every job of the new board.. Fix: Import must create companies inactive (spec) pending owner activation; add a DB-backed per-user import quota and a daily cap on new companies.
- [medium] Board and import fetches follow redirects and have no response-size cap: ats/types.ts:64-72 getJson uses the global fetch (redirect: follow) and res.json() with no byte cap; the URL host is fixed, but a redirect from the ATS or a very large body (Greenhouse content=true) can reach internal hosts or exhaust worker memory (4 concurrent x up to 24 MiB). Import fetches use the same path (job-import.ts:85).. Fix: Introduce safeFetch (no redirects, https and host allowlist, decompressed byte caps, connect-time address check) and use it for polls and imports.
- [medium] Prompt-injection surface: raw posting text goes to the scorer and nothing flags hostile postings: fit-scorer/render.ts:11-16 and fit.ts:113 send the description (fenced, up to 20k chars); no injection_suspect canary (matching-19); the score steers the feed and, later, Autopilot. A hostile posting can inflate its own score.. Fix: Send the jd-analyst card instead of raw text, add the ingest canary, make Autopilot refuse suspect jobs, and add the score-inflation alert (workers/03 §8.4).
- [low] User fact text sent to the model without contact redaction: score-matches.ts:51-58 builds the summary from raw facts.text; spec requires redactContact (workers/03 §3.3 C4, §10); no redaction in this path. Unverified: whether the resume parser already strips contact data from fact text.. Fix: Apply redactContact in the profile-block builder and cap to the top 25 confirmed facts.
- [low] Queue and skip routes lack state, job-open and cap guards: matches.ts:34-61: any status transitions, queueing a closed or ghost job, per-company cap not re-checked at write time (spec 04 §6.3 requires it). A user can exceed their own maxApplicationsPerCompanyPer30Days and employers may see duplicate approaches.. Fix: Add the status guard (user actor), closed-job and cap checks (P2 queueMatch).
- [low] last_poll_error stores raw exception text: poll-boards.ts:72 stores err.message, which includes full request URLs and zod issues. It is not user data, but it is stored unbounded and may be shown in admin tooling.. Fix: Store short error codes as in workers/01 §3.5.

**Spec gaps**

- Freshness targets contradict the cadences: a 30-minute interval gives about 32-minute p95 ingestion lag (target 30) and a 5-minute priority interval about 6.7 minutes (target 5). workers/01 §9.3 recommends 27 and 4, but 04 §23 still defaults 30 and 5. 04 is not amended.
- 04 §5.4 and §10.3 route backfill and re-scores to Message Batches 'if ZDR allows'. 03 §10.5 closes batch for fit-scorer (batchEligible 'never'), and workers/02 and 03 override 04. 04 and 01 B.7.9 still say otherwise.
- jd-analyst daily envelope is $8 base / $20 growth in 04 §3.2 but $11 in 03-agent-runtime.md; workers/02 adopts 03 without amending 04.
- 04 §2.4 bumps visible_at on any content_hash change and heartbeats last_seen_at on every poll. workers/01 replaces both (material/cosmetic rule, R-14). repost_pattern ownership differs between 04 §2.6 (ghost-signals) and 01 B.7.8 and workers/01 and 16 (ingest-poller). 04 is still unamended.
- next_match_at formula: 04 §5.2 and 01 B.7.3 say now()+cadence, which makes Max run every 10 minutes. workers/02 fixes it with tickStart + cadence - 30 s, but 04 and 01 are not amended. Rescore day accounting differs between 04 (next UTC day) and workers/02 and 03 (today).
- plans.ts and the spec disagree on the numbers: dailyModelSpendCapUsd is 0.5/2/5/8 in code and 0.05/0.20/0.25/0.50 in 04 §8.1. dailyScoringCap and matchCadenceMinutes do not exist in code. The cap formula in 04 §8.1 gives about 3 for Free (25 grants) but the table says 20.
- FR-53 priority definition is circular. Priority companies come from Max users' existing matches or applications, so a Max user's newly added target companies are polled at 30 minutes until a match exists, and the express lane misses their first postings. plans.priorityPolling (per-plan flag) is also unused, while the spec treats priority as a global company flag. 04 §5.3 ('at most 300 by the FR-53 cap logic of their activity') is unclear.
- F7 contradiction: teardown F7 wants ATS detection of arbitrary careers URLs (boardresolve), but B.8.6 forbids fetching the pasted URL and accepts only three ATS hostnames. Employer-domain links (?gh_jid=), which are very common, are unsupported and nothing says how to detect them safely.
- Match status machine is ambiguous. 02 R-18 says the system may move only among new, below_bar and expired, while workers/01 says expired to below_bar is not allowed. When preferences tighten and then widen, an `expired` match stays in the family exclusion (04 §2.6/6.2 NOT EXISTS on matches.family_id), so the job is never re-surfaced by backfill. Only below_bar returns to new (04 §12.1).
- Exploration sampling is underspecified. 04 §7.3 gives a 1% probability per gate-passed pair per run with at most one per user per day, while the cost line assumes about 1,000 calls a day. The ± 2 point recall interval in §14.4 depends on the unit and on the below-floor share of the sample, neither of which is stated.
- Ghost-job verdict `likely` hard-excludes a posting for all users, but outcome criteria depend on forwarding-address coverage ('no linked reply of any kind') and on two reporters, with no abuse model for collusion or for users who never forward mail. No remediation path is given for false positives.
- Prefilter cost claims rely on trigram indexes, but `location_lc LIKE '%ny%'` patterns can be 2 characters (preferencesSchema min 2), which trigram GIN cannot serve; the '1 to 5 ms on the window index' estimate holds only when the visible_at window drives the plan.
- Pre-rank half-credit for unconfirmed imported facts (04 §7.1) is justified as 'never shown, never sent to a model', yet it decides which pairs are scored. The truth-lock review should record this as an accepted exception.
- Company-cap statuses (04 reconciliation 3) and 'one match per family' are listed as owner questions inside 04 but the gate and prefilter text already assume the answers.
- No spec says how the initial 2,000 boards are sourced, how company names are resolved, or what the opt-out `/bot` page contains, although the user agent advertises it (ats/types.ts:67).

**Open questions**

- Is LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic on in staging/production today, and what has the scoring loop actually spent per day since beta users arrived? The env default is legacy (env.ts:48) and I did not read Railway variables.
- How many companies are in the live companies table and how were they loaded? The spec assumes about 2,000, and I found no seed script or admin route. Which hosts do they split across?
- Pin dailyModelSpendCapUsd and the new dailyScoringCap per plan: code says 0.5/2/5/8 dollars, spec says 0.05/0.20/0.25/0.50. Is drafting meant to share the pipeline cap?
- Do you want the interim controls (caps, eligibility, kill switch) shipped on the old loop now, ahead of the P4 build, as B.14 P1 requires before a paid beta?
- Confirm 04 §24: per-company cap counts only draft/approved/submitting/submitted; one match per job family (reposts allowed after 60 days?); thin-profile numbers (1 fact minimum, 15 scores a day until 8 facts); Starter cadence 60 vs 30 minutes; exploration at about $6 a month; the embeddings fallback staying in-process.
- Imported boards: create companies inactive (spec) so only the owner adds polled boards? Who works the board_requests queue? Should users be allowed to import employer-domain (gh_jid) links, which needs a safe resolve step that the spec currently forbids?
- Ashby isListed:false postings: ingest as 'unlisted' evidence (spec, owner confirm pending) or keep dropping them (current)?
- Sponsorship phrase list (OQ-13): approve the current regex or supply the list.
- Ingestion cadence: adopt 27-minute standard and 4-minute priority polling, or relax the p95 targets to 35 and 8 minutes?
- Priority polling: derive from Max users' matches (FR-53) or from users' stated target companies? Should the per-plan priorityPolling flag be the entitlement check?
- Short role names such as QA or PM currently match every title. Keep (spec pins it) or tighten the role matcher?
- Is the new-match email digest (F6) in the P4 scope or deferred as the PRD says?
- Who labels the S-F1 evaluation sets (500 pairs plus 100 personas) and when? The P4 shadow exit depends on them.
- Should the first additional ATS after Greenhouse, Lever and Ashby be chosen now (Workday via extension only, Smartrecruiters, others)? Is Phase 2 F8 still in plan?

    "Retry multiplier (queues.ts:40-44): up to 3x scorer spend on any failure, and below-threshold scores are never stored, so they are repaid.",
    "Greenhouse binding host at growth: roughly 3.5 average and 7 peak polls a second against the spec's 4 partitions (1.25x headroom), and today there is no partitioning at all, so a 429 storm stalls everything.",
    "Poll body size: 24 MiB x 8 concurrent can reach about 200 MB transient memory with no caps.",
    "Postgres: no cliff expected from matching itself (spec estimates under 1 vCPU), but the uncapped per-user count queries would reach about 120M a day at growth.",
    "Anthropic limits: Haiku RPM bucket (about 300 a minute per replica) is shared with drafting; uncapped scoring bursts can starve interactive features."
  ],
  "loadTestPlan": "1) Ingestion soak (P4 step 2): fake ATS serving recorded Greenhouse/Lever/Ashby listings for 2,000 then 10,000 companies, with ETag, 429 and truncated-body injection; measure poll lateness p95 (at most 2 min), ingest lag p95, jobs written per listing, zero closes on empty or truncated bodies. 2) Fan-out characterization of the CURRENT loop: seed 1k, 5k and 10k synthetic users and 500 jobs against a stub scorer (no real Anthropic), measure time per job, DB queries, and calls; use it to justify the interim cap and the cutover. 3) Matching v2: candidateTick at 2,850 eligible users (heaviest tick under 60 s, no page over 30 s), replay and kill tests (50 forced kills, zero duplicate matches). 4) 24-hour run at 1.5x growth volumes with stub Anthropic and recorded latencies (04 §19.3 / P8): feed lag by plan, cache share at or above 60%, scorer cost per submitted application within 20% of $0.0014. 5) Superset property test (10,000 fixtures) and S-F1 offline eval as CI gates.",
  "realityCheck": "The spec's capacity numbers are sound for the P4 design (about 115k scorer calls a day, 1.9 to 6.4 polls a second, under 1 vCPU of Postgres) but none of that design is running. Today's pipeline is a uncapped, event-driven fan-out sized for hundreds of users. The binding constraint is uncontrolled model spend and per-job latency, not infrastructure. The interim caps plus eligibility are the minimum needed to survive beta traffic, and the SQL prefilter plus cohort ticks are what actually unlock 10,000 users.",
  "sloProposal": [
    "Poll lateness p95 at most 2 minutes (both tiers); oldest due poll at most 10 minutes (priority) and 60 minutes (standard).",
    "Ingestion lag p95 at most 30 minutes standard and 5 minutes priority, which requires 27-minute and 4-minute cadences.",
    "Feed lag p95 after ingestion: Max 10 min (8 min end to end for priority via express), Pro 20 min, Starter and Free 65 min.",
    "candidateTick cohort drains in under 60 s; no candidatePage over 30 s; scoreUser run p95 at most 45 s; queue wait p95 at most 30 s (Max), 60 s (Pro), 5 min (Starter and Free).",
    "INV-1: zero fit-scorer or jd-analyst calls for gate-failing pairs (spy test); prefilter superset property: zero counterexamples in 10,000 fixtures.",
    "No job is closed from an empty or shrunken listing on the first poll; 100% of unchanged polls write zero jobs rows.",
    "Scoring cost per submitted application at most $0.0020 over 24 h (expected $0.0014); per-user spend never above plan cap; fewer than 0.1% of billed pairs paid twice.",
    "Quality: match_queue_rate_14d at least 0.70, prerank recall at least 0.85 weekly (0.98 offline floor recall); feed shows no NULL-score rows."
  ]
}
```

## messaging

Messaging is the thinnest domain relative to its spec. Built and wired: the per-user forwarding address (generated at sign-up), a token-gated Postmark inbound webhook that stores replies idempotently, a regex classifier with an offer guard, a basic substring linker, GET /api/inbox, a Postmark mailer used for OTP, and a contact form. Everything else is missing: encryption, size/spam/flood controls, the application stage and event ledger, notifications, Gmail confirmation handling, the reply-classifier agent, email_sends idempotency, the notify queue, digests, reminders, the weekly report, unsubscribe, and the two-stream Postmark setup. Worst gap: the live classify-email job still pauses the user's Stripe subscription immediately on any email whose text matches the offer regex, with no domain check, undo or notice (defect D-26 from as-built.md, still open). That makes billing pausable by anyone who learns a forwarding address. The webhook also parses up to 50 MB of JSON before checking the token. The spec leans on DBOS (ADR-001, still Proposed); none of that is installed. The pieces that do not need DBOS (schema, encryption, linker, stage, events, offer-candidate table, undo route) can be built first on BullMQ. The spec has internal contradictions (entity ids for forwarding_code and offer_pause_notice, the inbound domain, phase ordering of unsubscribe) and no agent specs for reply-classifier or outcome-analyst. The PRD also lists the weekly report as Built and the pricing page advertises it, but no report code exists.

**Risks**

- [critical/confirmed] Billing pause on any 'offer' text is still live (D-26 unfixed): server/src/jobs/classify-email.ts:34-40 calls pauseForOffer for every email tagged offer, with no link, domain, spam, undo or notification; offer regex matches bare 'job offer' (email-classifier.ts:8). Fix: Remove the direct pauseForOffer call immediately (S); ship the candidate + 48 h undo + notice path before re-enabling. Do not wait for DBOS.
- [high/confirmed] Entire messaging spec presumes DBOS, which is not installed and ADR-001 is unapproved: ADR-001 status Proposed; no @dbos-inc in server/package.json; BullMQ queues at server/src/jobs/queues.ts:11. Fix: Get the owner decision. Build schema, encryption, linker, stage, events, offer-candidate and undo as plain Postgres + BullMQ first so they port unchanged.
- [high/confirmed] Unsubscribe lands after the emails that need it: 12-engagement-notify.md s12: digest P4, Autopilot summary P5, unsubscribe and mail_opt_out P6; 05-api 12.1 marks unsubscribe P6. Fix: Move unsubscribe, mail_opt_out and broadcast stream to the same phase as the first non-transactional mail (P4).
- [medium] Gmail forwarding onboarding has no code surfacing: forwarding-address.tsx:40 tells users to confirm with Gmail's code; the confirmation mail is stored as a normal inbox row (webhooks.ts:82-86). Fix: Ship the confirmation parser and forwarding status panel early (needs S-G1). Interim: surface the code from the inbox preview or add a manual tip.
- [medium] Inbound failures are invisible and unrecoverable: webhooks.ts:87 enqueues only when a row is newly stored; no reconciler, no classified_at, no metrics. Fix: Add classified_at + a 5-minute redrive query before DBOS arrives; start workflow on duplicate deliveries too.
- [medium] Mislinking can attach replies and stages to the wrong application: classify-email.ts:26-27 substring match on company name over sender+subject+body; short names; ignores domain; companies has no domains column (jobs.ts:6-21). Fix: Tiered linker with word boundaries and length rule; populate companies.domains; keep Tier 3 link out of any money path.
- [medium] Product claims run ahead of code (weekly report, reply routing status): web/components/pricing/plan-comparison.tsx:25 lists 'Weekly outcome report'; PRD README:82 'Weekly report: Built'; no report code in server/src. Fix: Correct the PRD status, or hide the line until P6. Decide whether R4 Insights replaces the emailed report.
- [medium] Deliverability: OTP and future bulk mail share one Postmark stream: mailer.ts:30 MessageStream 'outbound'; env.ts has no broadcast token (06-infrastructure.md:63 X7). Fix: Create the broadcast server/stream and token before any digest; keep OTP on transactional.
- [low] Postmark volume and cost estimate disagree by 2x: 12-engagement-notify.md s9: about 213,000 messages a month ($260) vs 95,000 ($120) in 06-infrastructure s11.3. Fix: Reconcile; consider sending Autopilot summaries only at 2+ pending sends or after 3 h.
- [low] Contact form duplicates and lost founder alerts: contact.ts:14-20 insert then inline send; a send error returns 500 and the browser retry re-inserts. Fix: Dedupe by hash per UTC day, always answer 202, send alert through the queue with CONTACT_ALERT_TO.
- [medium] Email links need env values that are not defined: env.ts has no API_PUBLIC_ORIGIN, LINK_SIGNING_KEY, POSTMARK_BROADCAST_TOKEN, POSTMARK_INBOUND_* ; WEB_ORIGIN and BETTER_AUTH_URL unset on sd1-api. Fix: Add and validate these in parseEnv before building any link-bearing email; fix WEB_ORIGIN/BETTER_AUTH_URL so the inbox is reachable from Vercel.

**Security findings**

- [critical/confirmed] Anyone who learns a forwarding address can pause the user's subscription: classify-email.ts:34-40 and services/stripe.ts:47-52; addresses are in /api/me and visible in Gmail filter setup, and forwarded senders know it. Fix: Remove the direct pause now; require rule-tagged offer + domain link + submitted app + active subscription + 48 h undo; add pause_offer_candidates kill switch.
- [high/confirmed] Unauthenticated 50 MB JSON parse before the token check: webhooks.ts:71 express.json({limit:'50mb'}) is route middleware ahead of the handler's token check at :73; no rate limiter on /api/webhooks (app.ts:31-34). Fix: Check path secret/Basic in a middleware before parsing, add webhook_ip limiter, use the large-body permit gate, return 403 on auth failure.
- [high/confirmed] Third-party reply bodies stored in plaintext, unbounded, never purged: applications.ts:57 text_body notNull; webhooks.ts:84 stores full TextBody; no retention job. Fix: Encrypt body and preview (AES-GCM, row AAD), cap at 16 KB, add 180-day purge and 30-day hidden-row delete; split DB roles so the api cannot rewrite evidence.
- [medium] Inbound secret travels in the URL query: webhooks.ts:72 reads req.query.token; app logs redact it (webhook-hygiene.test.ts:7-9) but proxy and Railway/Postmark logs do not. Fix: Move to path secret + Basic credentials as specced; rotate INBOUND_WEBHOOK_SECRET after cut-over.
- [medium] No flood cap or spam handling on stored mail: webhooks.ts:82-86 inserts every message for a known address; no X-Spam-Score read. Fix: 400/day per-user cap, spam thresholds 5 and 8, restrict automation for suspect mail.
- [medium] Reply content can influence tags and a (future) model with no normalisation or fencing: email-classifier.ts:20 reads raw body including quoted history; no fence or redaction applied for inbox. Fix: Apply stripQuoted, then redactEmailBody and the gateway fence when the agent is added; keep the model unable to produce offer.
- [low] Contact form reaches the founder mailbox with attacker-controlled subject text and no dedupe: contact.ts:15-20; name inserted into subject; limiter is per-process memory (rate-limit.ts:12-17). Fix: Strip control characters, set Reply-To, dedupe by hash, Redis or DB-backed limit once more than one replica.
- [low] Dev mailer logs full email body (including OTP) when no token is set: mailer.ts:18-21; production guard only at env.ts:69-71. Fix: Confirm staging sets NODE_ENV=production or a token; drop text from the dev log line.
- [low] Constant-time compare leaks token length: webhooks.ts:17-21 checks length equality before timingSafeEqual. Fix: Hash both sides with SHA-256 first, as 05-api 14.3 specifies.

**Spec gaps**

- forwarding_code entity id is inconsistent: 10-inbound-email.md uses notify:forwarding_code:<inboundEmailId>, but 12-engagement-notify.md KIND_POLICY.entityPattern and _db/messaging.md 5.2 require <userId>:gmail:<epochSeconds>; the notifications entity differs too (<inboundEmailId> in 10 vs <receivedAtEpochSeconds> in messaging.md 5.3). notifyOne would reject the 10-style id.
- offer_pause_notice is keyed by <candidateId> in 10-inbound-email.md s3.2 but by <inboundEmailId> in 12 and messaging.md 5.2. 12 s1 and messaging.md say classifyEmail inserts an in-app offer_pause_notice notification, but 10 steps B6/B7 insert none.
- reply_important detail keys differ: messaging.md uses detail.replyKind, 10 B7 writes kind and hint.
- 05-api 14.3 step 6 gives the ingest input as {inboundEmailId} and parseGmailForwardingConfirmation without authResults; 10 adds route and a DKIM boolean (AR-3 and AR-1 are 'requests' not yet merged into 02-database/05-api).
- Inbound domain differs: replies.kairos.app (10, env default, auth.ts) vs reply.<domain> (06-infrastructure.md:197).
- Phase ordering: digests P4 and Autopilot summary P5 but unsubscribe, mail_opt_out and the broadcast stream P6. Also 10-inbound-email.md moves linking, stage and events from P6 to P2 against 01-architecture.md B.14 (called out only in s12).
- agents/reply-classifier.md and agents/outcome-analyst.md do not exist (agents/ holds only 01-resume-parser.md); AR-10 contract, the 400-case eval set and the 40 interview-offer cases are not defined anywhere. No master README for the spec.
- S-G1 is unrun: Gmail confirmation template, subject, link host, 9-digit code, X-Spam-Score header name and Authentication-Results presence in Postmark inbound JSON are all [unverified]. If Postmark does not forward Authentication-Results, the DKIM gate means the code is never shown.
- No consent model for non-transactional mail: default opt-in/out state, consent timestamp and source, physical address footer (CAN-SPAM), and authenticated preference route are unspecified; only token unsubscribe is.
- No handling for outbound bounces and spam complaints beyond reading a weekly Postmark dashboard; no durable per-user suppression.
- PRD vs spec mismatch: PRD stages (Saved, Drafting, Applied, Interviewing, Offer, Closed with reasons) vs spec applications.stage (applied, in_review, assessment, interviewing, offer, rejected); PRD maps assessment to Interviewing and has no in_review; mapping layer is unspecified. PRD says weekly report Built and digest Later, while the spec schedules the report P6 and the digest P4.
- PRD 'Add sender as contact' and 'This reply mentions an interview' need sender name and body text, but bodies are purged at 180 days and no retention rule covers the extracted fields.
- Duplicate forwards (same mail via two rules) are known to double count replies and are left unresolved (messaging.md s13 Q5).
- companies.domains has no owner or seed process (AR-2 is a request to other domains); Tier 2 ATS domain list is marked [verify on the beta sample].
- 12 s3.1 sub-step 4 relies on Postmark outbound metadata search, flagged [verify at P2]; the fallback (lease-only guard) permits duplicate emails, and no decision is recorded on whether that is acceptable for resend links.
- Web paths used in email templates (matches, queue, settings, billing) are not pinned; a contract test is deferred to implementation.

**Open questions**

- Approve ADR-001 (DBOS) now, or should the messaging P2 pieces ship first on BullMQ as an interim?
- Is it acceptable to disable the inline Stripe offer pause today, before the candidate + undo path exists, even though offer detection then only tags and notifies?
- Is Stripe live on any deployed environment? That decides whether D-26 is an active incident or a staging-only issue.
- On Railway staging: is the Postmark inbound server/MX configured for replies.kairos.app, is the webhook pointed at /api/webhooks/inbound-email?token=, and which Postmark tokens (transactional, broadcast) and plan features (metadata search, two servers) exist?
- Final inbound domain: replies.kairos.app or reply.<domain>?
- Should digests and the weekly report be on or off by default, and what is the consent basis and sender postal address for CAN-SPAM, GDPR and DPDP?
- Move the crypto helper (inbound body encryption) to P1/P2 as messaging.md s13 Q1 recommends, accepting about one extra day?
- Confirm spam thresholds (restrict at 5, hide at 8) and whether hiding at 8 is acceptable.
- Should a domain-linked confirmation email for an application in failed/unconfirmed ever trigger the late-confirmation charge (O-2), or only notify?
- Do you want a self-serve route to rotate a leaked forwarding address in v1 (O-3), or manual support?
- Does the weekly outcome report ship as an email (P6, Pro/Max) as the spec says, or only as R4 Insights? The PRD calls it Built and the pricing page advertises it.
- How should reply kinds map to the PRD tracker stages (assessment to Interviewing? confirmation to nothing?), and should sender name and interview hints be kept after the 180-day body purge?
- Retention periods (180 d bodies, 120 d email_sends, 90 d notifications, 12 mo contact): counsel sign-off?
- Which Postmark cost lever do you prefer for Autopilot summaries (hourly vs 2+ pending or 3 h wait), given about $260 a month vs $120 planned?
- What address receives contact-form alerts (CONTACT_ALERT_TO) and the owner alerts (ALERT_EMAIL_TO)?

**Capacity**

Mail bodies up to 16 KB stored; webhook payloads usually under 200 KB but up to 35 MB with attachments.",
  "currentCapacity": "One Express api handler per request plus a BullMQ worker with concurrency 4 for classify-email (worker.ts:17). Per mail: 1 recipient lookup, 1 insert, 1 enqueue, then 2 to 3 queries and an optional Stripe call. Rules-only path is cheap, so 0.1 to 0.4 mails/s is far under capacity. Outbound is inline per request (OTP, contact) with no queue, no retry and no timeout.",
  "perTier": [
    "api: body parsing, not CPU, is the limit; one 35 MB attachment mail costs about 100 to 150 MB transient heap, so 3 concurrent large posts on a 0.75 GB replica can OOM (and the parse happens before auth).",
    "worker: classify concurrency 4 handles about 60 to 200 mails/s rules-only; adding the model path (about 1.5 s) caps at about 5/s per 8 slots, still above the 0.36/s peak.",
    "postgres: about 25 write statements a minute for the domain at target volume; trivial. inbound_emails grows about 3 million rows a year, with plaintext bodies today (no purge).",
    "postmark: 2/s peak is within normal limits; the risk is reputation and stream mixing, not rate."
  ],
  "cliffs": [
    "50 MB JSON body parsed before auth: memory exhaustion at a few concurrent requests.",
    "Replay storm after an api outage (Postmark retries about 3,500 mails in an hour) is fine for rules-only but would need the model deferral path once the agent exists.",
    "In-memory express-rate-limit is per replica; the contact limit and any future webhook limit multiply with replicas.",
    "A digest or report burst with no queue and no priority would delay OTP and forwarding codes if sent inline.",
    "No retention: unbounded growth of inbound bodies (about 5 GB per 180 days at target) in plaintext."
  ],
  "loadTestPlan": "Replay 1.5x the busiest hour (about 1,900 fixtures) through the inbound route against staging with a stub Anthropic and Postmark sandbox; include 5 posts of 20 to 35 MB with attachments; verify S8 (99% classified within 10 min), zero duplicate rows or events on 10 concurrent redeliveries, and api RSS under 70% of the replica. Then a 1,000-user outbound burst (Monday report) to confirm OTP latency stays under 30 s at p95.",
  "realityCheck": "Real reply volume is unknown (the 0.4 mails per submitted application figure is an estimate); the rule-tag share (75%) and ATS-relayed spam scores (2 to 4) are unmeasured, and many replies come from ATS domains that Tier 2 only partly resolves. Measure on the first 14 days of beta mail.",
  "sloProposal": [
    "S8: 99% of inbound mail classified within 10 minutes (spec).",
    "Inbound acceptance: 99.9% of Postmark deliveries answered 200 within 2 s (excluding replays).",
    "Transactional email (OTP, forwarding code, offer notices): 95% handed to Postmark within 30 s of the trigger.",
    "Offer-pause: zero pauses without a pending candidate older than 48 h and an unclicked undo link (audit weekly)."
  ]
}
```

## ops

Operations is almost entirely unbuilt. The server has pino logging with secret redaction, a /health probe, a Railway pre-deploy migrate, BullMQ's own stalled-job handling, and a gated LLM ledger. The ledger is llm_calls plus the cost-rollup, partition and budget SQL and TypeScript from migration 0002. That is the whole ops footprint. There is no metrics table, error tracker, tracing, alerting, ops_flags/ops_audit, scheduler for the 0002 SQL functions, backup or PITR config, retention job, erasure pipeline, runbook, chaos harness or rehearsal code. Two things already bite on today's stack. (1) Migration 0002 created llm_calls partitions for 7 weeks only. Nothing calls llm_calls_ensure_partitions or llm_cost_rollup_day, so partitions run out around mid-December 2026 and the budget envelopes and shed levels read an empty llm_cost_daily; both matter once LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic is switched on. (2) A cloud-apply claim moves an application to 'submitting' (services/submissions.ts:52), and nothing ever settles it if the applier dies, so a credit stays held. Account deletion cancels Stripe subscriptions and cascades rows, but it destroys the credit ledger and leaves no tombstone. No row-retention code exists at all. The specs are detailed and mostly consistent. They hold real contradictions (retention schedule times, rehearsal status model, userHash definition, admin resume of applyCloud, SLO writer ownership). They also assume ADR-001 (DBOS), which is still Proposed. Rehearsal (F9) is listed as a non-goal in the resume-suite PRD, so it is a P6 item with no PRD release. Recommendation: before ADR-001 lands, ship a small interim ops set on the Express/BullMQ stack (see capacity.realityCheck).

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] llm_calls partition runway ends about 7 weeks after migration 0002: server/drizzle/0002_platform_02_llm_ledger.sql:139-157,260 creates current week +6 once; no caller of llm_calls_ensure_partitions in server/src. The function raises if llm_calls_default holds rows.. Fix: Add a daily BullMQ job calling llm_calls_ensure_partitions(6) and a default-partition row-count check now. High only when LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic; confirm the flag state.
- [high/confirmed] Budget envelopes and shed levels read an empty llm_cost_daily: server/src/llm/spend-store.ts:13-51 reads llm_cost_daily; llm_cost_rollup_day (0002:203) is never called; shedLevel fixed at 0 (budget.ts:67); gateway default legacy (env.ts:48).. Fix: Schedule llm_cost_rollup_day every minute before enabling the gateway in production; wire llm_shed_level from ops_flags.
- [high/confirmed] No monitoring, error tracking or alerting while real money and real submissions flow: No metrics, sentry, tracing, alerts or worker/applier health in server/src or package.json; as-built.md:27,335; server/src/routes/health.ts covers api only.. Fix: Ship the interim set: external uptime + heartbeat, Sentry, worker/applier liveness, Stripe-event and stuck-submitting age gauges, before paid beta.
- [high/confirmed] Stuck submitting applications hold credits forever after an applier crash: server/src/services/submissions.ts:52-60 claim; services/credits.ts:19 counts submitting as in-flight; no lease/sweeper in server/src; BullMQ redelivery skips because status is no longer approved (cloud-apply.ts:36-38).. Fix: Add a settle-or-fail sweeper (stale submitting older than N minutes to needs_check/failed with release) on the current stack; later ops-18.
- [high/confirmed] No verified backups, PITR or restore drill: No backup service, R2 client, ops/ directory or runbook in repo; server/railway/*.json has no postgres config.. Fix: Confirm Railway PITR on staging and prod, add a nightly encrypted pg_dump of the core tables now, and run one restore drill before paid beta.
- [medium] ADR-001 not approved: durable-ops (14) and DBOS-hosted schedules (15, 16) are speculative: 01-architecture ADR-001 status Proposed; resume-suite PRD D7 says server work continues on today's Express/BullMQ.. Fix: Define an ops-lite scope on BullMQ that is also correct under DBOS (same SQL functions, flags, metrics tables) so work is not thrown away.
- [medium] Operator resume route could double-submit applyCloud: 05-api.md:1790 says applyCloud is safe to resume; 14-durable-ops s3.2/s3.4 and R-DBOS-1 say never resume past runBrowser.. Fix: Make admin.workflows.resume refuse applyCloud and route through attempt-phase settlement.
- [medium] Monitoring would not catch the known sign-in failure: 06 6.7 external checks hit /health and /api/health only; env.ts:14-15 requires BETTER_AUTH_URL and WEB_ORIGIN yet the task states they are unset on sd1-api.. Fix: Add a synthetic auth-origin probe (OPTIONS/CORS + sign-in page reachability) to the external monitor and fix the values.
- [medium] No row retention: tables grow unbounded: No delete/retention code in server/src; matches below_bar about 150,000 rows/day at growth (16 s3.4 R01); inbound email text stored forever (0000_init.sql:167).. Fix: Ship the inbound-email body purge and matches below_bar rule first; they only need current schema.
- [medium] Specified ops stack is heavy for a solo operator: about 5,170 scheduled workflows/day from 15 s9, 13 schedules, about 50 runbooks (06 s10), page budget 2/week.. Fix: Phase by blast radius: money/duplicate-send invariants and backups first; defer shadow audit, canaries, ghost and rehearsal.
- [medium] Metrics pipeline assumption breaks across replicas: 16 s8 says counters are written by the metrics schedule from in-process counters, but the schedule and each housekeeping run execute on different replicas.. Fix: Have each job write its own metrics_minute rows (or a small run-result table) instead of in-process counters.
- [low] Rehearsal conflicts with PRD scope and plan messaging: Resume-suite PRD README:70,113 lists interview rehearsal as non-goal/Later; 05-api.md:1287 says Starter and up while plans.ts gives Starter 0.. Fix: Treat F9 as post-R4; fix the 05-api text to generate from plans.ts.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Account erasure is incomplete and destroys the financial audit trail: server/src/lib/auth.ts:47-63 cancels subscriptions then relies on FK cascade; server/src/db/schema/billing.ts:14 credit_ledger.user_id ON DELETE CASCADE; no tombstone, no Stripe customer deletion, no contact_messages (0000_init.sql:180), llm_calls.user_hash scrub, or R2 cleanup.. Fix: Implement deletion_tasks, ledger archive and tombstone first (ops-51/52/53); a PITR or dump restore currently resurrects deleted users.
- [medium] Personal data kept indefinitely (inbound email bodies, contact messages, matches, jobs): No retention job in server/src; server/drizzle/0000_init.sql:167 inbound_emails text stored forever (as-built S-12); contact_messages keeps name/email/message (billing.ts:55-62).. Fix: Build the retention engine and R05/R06/R16 rules; document periods in the privacy notice.
- [medium] Log redaction narrower than the spec; raw error objects are logged: server/src/lib/logger.ts:10-20 redacts auth/cookie/token/code only; worker.ts:22 and applier.ts:15 log full err; spec 06 6.2 requires *.text, *.content, *.answers, err.response.body etc. and a pii-scan CI job.. Fix: Adopt the spec's redaction list and add the seeded-PII test before enabling the gateway and rehearsal.
- [low] userHash defined two ways (unkeyed SHA-256 of user id vs HMAC with USER_HASH_KEY): 16 s2 ErasureTaskInput and X2 use sha256(user id); 06 6.2 and 03 s10.2 use HMAC; tombstones are kept forever.. Fix: Use the keyed HMAC everywhere, including tombstones, and document key rotation impact.
- [low] Unauthenticated /health lists dependency names and status: server/src/routes/health.ts:20 returns checks {database, redis}.. Fix: Return only status to the public; keep detail behind the admin route.
- [low] Rehearsal answers stored plaintext for 30 days and sent to the model with free-text sensitive content: 13 s10 (plain columns, redactContact cannot remove visa or salary text).. Fix: Encrypt answer/feedback columns with DATA_KEKS or shorten retention; keep the UI warning.
- [low] Local Redis snapshot present in server working directory: server/dump.rdb exists; ignored by .gitignore:14 so not committed.. Fix: Delete it and keep Redis persistence out of the working tree.
- [low] Operator control plane (ops_flags, admin routes) is a freeze/unfreeze attack surface once built: 06-infrastructure s10 / 15 s10: flags writable by kairos_api and kairos_worker; admin routes gated by ADMIN_USER_IDS and fresh session.. Fix: Keep audit-by-trigger and the evaluator alert on flags set outside system/operator set_by; test the 404 on non-admin.

**Spec gaps**

- No master README; ADR-001 is Proposed. If the owner rejects DBOS, 14-durable-ops is void and 15/16 need a different host. No spec says what ops looks like on today's BullMQ stack, which PRD decision D7 keeps for R1-R4.
- Retention schedule time conflicts: 13 s1/s2.1 says daily 03:00 UTC, 16 s2 says 30 7 * * * (07:30 UTC), 14 puts dbos-retention at 03:30 UTC.
- Rehearsal expiry data model conflicts: 16 R11 and s6 use rehearsal_sessions.status active to expired; 13 and 02 use ended_at plus end_reason.
- 05-api.md:1287 says rehearsal is on Starter and up; plans.ts gives Starter 0 (13 AD-R3 flags it; 05 not yet fixed). 05 also lists only Free as 403.
- interview-coach timeoutMs: 13 AD-R6 requires 25 s, 03 says 60 s or 30 s; deadline 55 s and the shed-level behaviour (429 service_budget vs 503) in 05-api 20.2 are still unreconciled.
- Scheduled-workflow id conventions differ: 14 uses name:<epoch>, 15 uses mon:<monitor>:<period>, 16 uses scheduler-derived sched-<name>-<time>. 15 s13.9 admits it is unverified that a scheduled body can set an explicit workflowID.
- 05-api.md:1790 (applyCloud safe to resume) contradicts 14 s3.2/3.4 and R-DBOS-1/2 (never resume or fork applyCloud past the browser step).
- checkpoint_writes_per_submitted is computed two ways (14 s3.7 pg_stat_user_tables diff vs 15 s3.2 queue_depth_v companion view); the schedule is called metrics in 06 and 14 but metricsTick in 15.
- 16 s8 says housekeeping metrics are written by the metrics schedule from in-process counters; schedules run on rotating replicas, so those counters cannot be collected. Unbuildable as written.
- SLO counters slo.<id>.good/total, multi-window burn-rate rules and the error-budget policy (06 6.6) have no owning worker spec; several SLIs (S4, S5, S7, S8) depend on tables not yet created.
- backup.last_success_age_s for core and full dumps, the weekly manifest row-count check and the drill-overdue alert A-DATA-3 have no owner (15 writes only the journal kind; 06 5.3 refers to a weekly check in 6.7 that does not exist).
- 06 s6.7 alert catalogue lacks A-DUR-8, A-DUR-9, A-INV-1, A-LLM-7 and A-DATA-4..6 that workers 13-16 request. 13 puts three different rules under one id A-LLM-7 and maps retention lag to an 'A-DATA family' while 16 requests new ids.
- Erasure steps X2, X4 and X10 need the user id but deletion_tasks holds only hashes; 16 s13.1 admits it needs a user_id column or encrypted input. X10 verification after the user id is deleted is not implementable as written.
- Ownership conflicts: 14 s1 leaves identity reconcilers (sign-up without profile) with identity, 15 s12 says durable-ops owns them; 16 s13.2 notes scrub-applications and failed-expiry have no owning worker.
- I-9 (questions_asked equals max turn seq over 30 days) is undefined once retention deletes turns older than 30 days; false positives on ended sessions.
- Shadow audit sample rate: 01/03 say 2% weekly for about $10; 15 s13.1 shows 2% at growth is about 25x that, so it caps at 320 calls/day (about 0.08%). Needs owner decision and doc edits.
- Retention batch cap: 14 A-1 needs 400 batches/night, 02 s12.2 and 06 R-DBOS-5 still say 100.
- Resume-suite PRD lists interview rehearsal as a non-goal / Later; no PRD release R1-R4 carries it, while 13 schedules it at P6.
- 06 s6.2 says pino-http samples 2xx and logs slow requests; 13/14 assume requestId and workflowId on every line; no spec defines the shared logger bootstrap contract for the worker and applier.

**Open questions**

- Do you approve ADR-001 (DBOS on Postgres)? If not, 14-durable-ops must be rewritten and 15/16 re-hosted. Should the resume-suite releases ship with an interim BullMQ ops set meanwhile?
- Is LLM_GATEWAY=anthropic live on Railway staging or any production environment today? That sets how urgent the partition cron and cost rollup are.
- Are Railway Postgres PITR and any dumps enabled on kairos-sd1-staging, and is there a production environment yet? Will you accept paid beta with no tested restore?
- What paging channel and response time can you commit to (06 Q3), who holds the second copy of DATA_KEKS and the backup key (Q12), and is the Sentry $50/mo cap and about $25/mo phone paging approved?
- Shadow audit budget: accept the 320 claims/day cap (about 0.08% effective, $10/week) or fund the full 2%?
- Rehearsal: Pro and up (plans.ts) or Starter included? Should F9 ship before paid beta given the PRD lists it as a non-goal? Encrypt rehearsal answers at rest?
- Retention periods and the erasure SLA (inbound email 180 d, matches below_bar 30 d, jobs 180 d, 24 h erasure page, 28-day backup tail): confirmed with counsel?
- Erasure design: add deletion_tasks.user_id (16 s13.1) or pass an encrypted user id into the workflow? Confirm the keyed HMAC as the only userHash.
- What are WEB_ORIGIN and BETTER_AUTH_URL actually set to on sd1-api (env.ts:14-15 makes them required)? Which providers must work at launch, so the synthetic probe covers them?
- Should operator actions (workflow resume/fork, ledger repair) exist as HTTP admin routes at all before P2, or only as audited CLI scripts (05-api A-16)?

**Capacity**

## platform

Platform runtime today is the pre-ADR skeleton: three Node entry points (api, worker, applier) on BullMQ/Redis, postgres.js, one flat zod env schema, pino logging, Railway railway.json files, one CI workflow. Nothing from ADR-001's platform layer is built: no DBOS or durable seam, no pg driver or role split, no per-process env schemas, no R2 client, no Sentry/OTel/metrics/alerts, no worker/applier health or timed drain, no egress guard, no IaC, no promotion pipeline, no backups beyond whatever Railway gives by default. Genuinely built: fail-fast env validation, pino redaction with a test, esbuild bundle, a CI typecheck/lint/test/build plus Docker build gate, Phase A LLM gateway code (flagged off by default, LLM_GATEWAY=legacy), an LLM ledger migration (0002), a claim-then-submit fix for the withdraw race, an atomic Stripe event transaction, and DB-backed integration tests that CI never runs (no Postgres service). Biggest platform risks: the DBOS hard gate (S-D1 Railway half) is unproven; applier has no SSRF/egress guard and runs Chromium without a sandbox next to the private network; every process holds one all-powerful DB credential; `submitting` can stick forever (no sweeper); approve-then-enqueue is non-atomic; IP-keyed rate limits likely collapse behind the Vercel to Railway proxy chain; the resume-suite PRD (D7: build on today's Express/BullMQ) conflicts with ADR-001's DBOS port and with migration numbering. The spec has real internal drift (partitionQueue API, PgBouncer connection math, checkpoint-write counts, beta-gate week) that 06 and the spike patch but 01 does not.

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] DBOS hard gate unproven; platform dependency for P2-P6: spike-s-d1-local.md:33-38 Railway half open (executor ids across redeploy, PgBouncer datasource, 50 crashes); 01 A.5 and R1. Fix: Run the Railway half of S-D1 before writing any workflow code beyond the seam; pre-agree the pg-boss swap trigger so a failed spike costs days, not a re-plan.
- [high/confirmed] submitting can stick forever; no sweeper or timeout: jobs/cloud-apply.ts:35-37 skips when claim fails; services/submissions.ts:49-59 claim; no reconciler anywhere; credits.ts:19 counts submitting as in-flight. Fix: Ship a minimal stuck-submitting sweeper on the current BullMQ stack now (cheap), ahead of the P2 attempt protocol, so staging users do not lose available credits.
- [high/confirmed] Approve and other writes enqueue after commit (lost follow-up work): routes/applications.ts:94; routes/matches.ts:59; routes/profile.ts:62; routes/webhooks.ts:87. Fix: Add the cheap reconciler for approved-cloud-without-job and draft-without-tailor before P2; this is the D1/D2 rule of the target design.
- [high/confirmed] Applier can reach the private network and runs Chromium without sandbox flags: jobs/cloud-apply.ts:19-21,49-51 (no proxy, no host allowlist, bypassCSP, Playwright default chromiumSandbox=false); apply_url comes from ATS payloads. Fix: Bring forward the egress guard and APPLIER_ALLOWED_HOSTS from P5 for any real traffic; decide S-AP4 sandbox early.
- [high/confirmed] One shared all-powerful DB credential and one env for api, worker and applier: db/client.ts:13-15; env.ts single schema; no ops/ dir. Fix: Land roles and envFor(role) in P1 before the applier handles real users; a compromised browser process today holds the ledger, sessions and every user's data.
- [high/confirmed] P1 is overloaded in the spec: 2 weeks for DBOS seam, PG18+PITR, pg driver swap, PgBouncer, roles, gateway, R2, Sentry, metrics, alerts, Grafana, pgbackup, IaC: 01 B.14 P1 (2 wk) vs 06 12.1 P1 deliverables list. Fix: Split P1 into P1a (database + roles + driver + DBOS seam + S-D1) and P1b (observability, backups, R2, IaC); re-baseline the 16-week figure.
- [medium] IP-keyed rate limits behind the Vercel rewrite probably collapse to one bucket: app.ts:22 trust proxy 1; middleware/rate-limit.ts:7-12 (120/min per IP); auth.ts:62 Better Auth limiter; web/next.config.ts:27-29. Fix: Probe the XFF chain through the real rewrite (S-INF2/S-B1) and make hop count env-driven; until then all web users may share 120 requests/min and 30 auth calls/min.
- [medium] postgres.js behind PgBouncer transaction mode without prepare:false: db/client.ts:14 default prepared statements; PgBouncer service exists on sd1; DATABASE_URL target unknown. Fix: Confirm which URL sd1-api/worker use; set prepare:false or enable max_prepared_statements; use the unpooled URL for migrations and (later) DBOS.
- [medium] Railway railway.json stops being read on 2026-12-01: 06 0.1 (V); server/railway/*.json are the only deploy config. Fix: Import current settings into IaC or the dashboard before that date; low effort, high blast radius if missed.
- [medium] Money and webhook invariants are not exercised in CI: test/helpers/test-db.ts:14; ci.yml has no Postgres service; int tests skipIf(!hasTestDb). Fix: Add a Postgres service (PG18) to ci.yml now; it is the P0 exit and prerequisite for every later phase.
- [medium] No error tracking, metrics or alerting while a staging beta runs: no Sentry/OTel/metrics in server/src or package.json; health only checks db+redis. Fix: Land Sentry and the uptime monitor first (small), before the full metrics/alert stack.
- [medium] Worker and applier shutdown unbounded; api exits without draining: api.ts:28-30; worker.ts:32; applier.ts:20; no RAILWAY_DEPLOYMENT_DRAINING_SECONDS. Fix: Add deadlines (25/30/170 s) and set the Railway drain variables; today a deploy can SIGKILL a mid-submit browser.
- [medium] Dual plan collision: resume-suite PRD builds on BullMQ and migration numbers 0003+, ADR-001 replaces both: PRD D7 and section 12; 02-database.md:2702 numbers 0004-0012; routes/profile.ts:62. Fix: Owner decides whether R1-R4 are built before P3 (then ported) or on DBOS; reserve migration numbers explicitly.
- [medium] Phase A gateway is unvalidated against the live API and off by default: env.ts:48 LLM_GATEWAY default legacy; phase-a plan 2026-10-24: no live Anthropic call has run. Fix: Run S-A1 smoke on staging before P3 builds on it; cache-share and rate-limit assumptions rest on it.
- [medium] Redis queue durability not asserted (noeviction, persistence) while it carries money-adjacent jobs: queues.ts:27 no config check; as-built S-10. Fix: Verify the Railway Redis service policy now; low effort and removes a silent loss path until P7.
- [low] Spec drift between 01 and the spike/06 will mislead implementers: 01 B.5.3 partitionQueue/concurrency; B.5.5 GC call; B.5.7 751k writes vs 06 1.11M; 01 B.4.2 108 vs 06 134 vs 02 178 connections; 01 beta gate week 13.5 vs summed 12.5. Fix: Fold the spike corrections and 06 deltas into 01 or write the missing README master that names the winner per number.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Cloud applier has no SSRF or egress control: server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:49-51 page.goto(row.applyUrl) in a context with bypassCSP:true; apply_url from Lever/Ashby payloads is used as given (as-built P-04); services share the Railway private network. Fix: Egress proxy + exact ATS host allowlist + private/link-local/CGNAT/IPv6-ULA block + loopback bypass flag (06 7.5), before any untrusted URL reaches production.
- [high/confirmed] Chromium launched with Playwright defaults (sandbox off) with full-rights credentials in the same container: cloud-apply.ts:21 chromium.launch without args; Dockerfile.applier runs as pwuser but env holds DATABASE_URL, BETTER_AUTH_SECRET, STRIPE and others (env.ts:10-23). Fix: S-AP4: run sandboxed as non-root or accept risk explicitly; strip secrets from the applier (envFor(role)) and use the kairos_applier role.
- [high/confirmed] No database roles: every process uses the same superuser-class credential, ledger not append-only at DB level: db/client.ts:13; no provision-roles.sql; credit_ledger UPDATE/DELETE not revoked (02-database 4.x requires it). Fix: ops/provision-roles.sql + grants migration in P1; per-process URLs; REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE on credit_ledger.
- [medium] Per-IP rate limiting likely ineffective or over-aggressive behind the proxy chain: app.ts:22; middleware/rate-limit.ts; Better Auth limiter auth.ts:62. Fix: Env-driven proxy hops, verify XFF through Vercel, move limiter to shared Postgres store and key authenticated traffic by user id.
- [medium] Inbound email webhook authenticates with a query-string secret: routes/webhooks.ts token query; INBOUND_WEBHOOK_SECRET in env.ts:23; only the app log is redacted (logger.ts:38), not Railway edge or Postmark logs. Fix: Move to path secret + Basic credentials per 06 4.2 with rotation overlap.
- [medium] OAuth access/refresh/id tokens stored in clear in the account table; no app-layer encryption: auth.ts has no token-encryption option; as-built P-03; DATA_KEKS absent from env.ts. Fix: Better Auth token encryption or a Drizzle wrapper (S-B1); DATA_KEKS for resume sources and email bodies in P6.
- [medium] Floating base images, caret dependency ranges and npm lifecycle scripts in image builds: Dockerfile:8,34 node:22-slim; Dockerfile.applier:36 playwright tag only; package.json:23-40; Dockerfile:15,25 npm ci without --ignore-scripts. Fix: Digest pins, save-exact, --ignore-scripts + explicit rebuild, bundle hash proof.
- [low] GitHub Actions pinned by mutable tag; no secret scanning or dependency audit in CI: .github/workflows/ci.yml:23-26,44-48 actions/*@v5, docker/*@v3/v6; permissions already contents: read. Fix: Pin by commit SHA; add gitleaks and osv-scanner jobs.
- [low] Logs can still carry free-text fields and arbitrary query params: logger.ts:10-20 redact list lacks *.text/*.messages/*.body; redactUrl handles token and code only. Fix: Adopt the 06 6.2 redaction list and cut query strings; add the log-buffer scan to the PII test.
- [low] No secret rotation procedures or emergency kit exist for the secrets that will be unrecoverable (DATA_KEKS, backup age key): none in repo; 06 4.5-4.6. Fix: Write the kit and rotation runbooks before P6 encryption lands.

**Spec gaps**

- P0-P8 SUMMARY (01-architecture.md B.14 is the only plan; there is no README master). P0 Safety net and spikes, 1 wk: recorded contract suite S-C1 from web/lib/api.ts, web/hooks/*.ts, extension/src/lib/api.ts; Testcontainers PG18 harness; invariant tests (charge once, approve race, one grant per invoice, Stripe redelivery, nothing sent before approval, no model call on hard-filter fail or sensitive question); exact pins; start S-D1/S-P1/S-A1/S-B1. Exit: 41 existing tests plus new ones green on the skeleton, tests for D-07, D-08, D-13, D-14 committed as expected failures. Today: partial (contract level 1, three int suites that CI skips, no pins).
- P1 Foundations, 2 wk: PG18 + PITR, pg driver, PgBouncer, roles and per-process creds, DBOS seam with kairos_dbos/heartbeats/orphan recovery/version rule/retention carrying no traffic, Anthropic gateway replacing chatJson, llm_calls/llm_cost_daily/user_daily_usage/ai_usage, plans.ts fields, interim caps on the old scoring loop, Postgres rate limiter, Postmark path secret and URL redaction. Flag LLM_GATEWAY. Exit: S-D1, S-P1, S-A1, S-B1 passed; 100% of model calls through gateway with a cost row; caps enforced on old loop (spy test); restore drill timed. Today: only gateway/ledger code built; rest missing.
- P2 Money, sending and truth on DBOS, 2 wk: credit_balances + triggers, status guard, ledger indexes/sign check, draft_state, fact fixes D-03..D-05, approveCore, request workflows (queueMatch, approve, resend), applyCloud with attempt protocol up to filling, settleAttempt, recordOutcome, extension lease/report rules, claim routes, resend/signed links, withdraw guard, Stripe ingest/process workflows, offer-pause candidates, inbound email (rules), application_events; BullMQ cloud-apply and classify-email drain. Flag LIFECYCLE_V2 per user. Exit: chaos test of 1000 approvals with random kills and one Postgres restart: all terminal within 10 min, zero duplicate attempts, zero double charges; contract suite green; known-defect tests green.
- P3 Verifier, prepare v2 and workspace backend, 3 wk: jd-analyst/job cards, F1, checkClaims, claim-verifier (+escalation, 7-day shadow), drafting v2, form_questions, prepareApplication/reverifyDraft on DBOS, sync PATCH verifier, Typst renderer + render cache + application PDFs, resumes/revisions, employments/provenance, parseResume on DBOS, all workspace routes, F11 allowances; tailor-application and parse-resume queues drain. Flags PREPARE_V2, WORKSPACE_API. Exit: S-A2 and S-T1 pass; zero unsupported claims in 7-day sample; false-reject <= 8%; draft ready p95 < 60 s; workspace contract tests green.
- P4 Matching v2, 2.5 wk: polling on DBOS (ETag, batches of 25, per-host partitions, hints, canary, visible_at), ghost evidence F2, cohort ticks, GatePassed, lazy cards, F1 pre-rank, pacing/caps/demand budget, scoreUser, backfill, nightly re-score, saved searches/digests F6; 3-day shadow into matches_shadow; poll-boards and score-matches drain. Flag MATCHING_V2 by cohort. Exit: S-F1 and S-I1 pass; shadow reproduces >= 90% of old above-bar matches; scoring calls/day within 20% of cost model; superset property test green.
- P5 Applier hardening then Autopilot, 2 wk: egress guard, host allowlist, applier role only, submit_seen (S-AP2), challenge detection, extension-only switch, browser recycling, scanForm, sizing (S-AP1); then Autopilot settings/guards/canary/spread delays/spike pause/freeze/notifications/off switch, opening only after 14 days of verifier enforcement with shadow audit <= 1%. Flags CLOUD_APPLY_V2, AUTOPILOT. Exit: 50 forced crashes with zero duplicate submissions and zero charges for unreported attempts; SSRF suite blocked with no breakage; Autopilot chaos: off switch during a burst returns unclaimed approvals to draft and releases credits.
- Paid beta gate (after P5, stated as about week 13.5). Contradiction: summing P0..P5 durations (1+2+2+3+2.5+2) gives 12.5 weeks, not 13.5; 06 12.1 adds extra gate items (R-DR-1/2 rehearsed, rotation done on staging, Anthropic tier decision, counsel review) that carry no duration.
- P6 Billing, replies, privacy and engagement, 2 wk: rollover expiry, daily billing reconciliation, offer-pause executor, Gmail verification (S-G1), reply-classifier, domain-first linking, reminders F5, report route, notifications, deletion tasks/erasure/tombstones, raw-source and sensitive-answer encryption, outcome-analyst, interview rehearsal F9, retention/partition schedules. Exit: billing integrity dashboard clean 14 days on Stripe test clocks; recorded Stripe replay yields identical ledgers; erasure drill within 24 h incl. restore with tombstone replay. Gate: before 1,000 active users. (06 12.1 pulls deletion/erasure into P2; 01 keeps it in P6.)
- P7 Decommission Redis/BullMQ, 0.5 wk: confirm six skeleton queues drained, remove bullmq/ioredis/express-rate-limit/postgres, delete Redis service; exit: no Redis/BullMQ reference in server/src, infra bill matches B.12.2. P8 Load and launch gates, 1 wk: 24 h load at 1.5x with stub Anthropic and fake ATS, restore drill, Anthropic rate-limit and ZDR addendum in writing, 14 days of beta data replacing every [est]; exit: B.12 numbers reproduced within 25%, connection budget held. Gate: before public beta at 10,000 users. Phase 2 (F8 adapters, DOCX/templates, embeddings/Conductor triggers) unscheduled.
- There is no master README: no single place states the approved decision, the phase calendar, owners per phase, or the precedence rule (06 says the spike table overrides 01, but 01 itself was not corrected). ADR-001 status is Proposed, so P1 cannot formally start.
- 01 B.5.3 queue options are not buildable as written on DBOS 5.3.0: partitionQueue does not exist, concurrency is a deprecated alias, queues register after launch (spike table). 01 B.5.5 retention names a GC function that does not exist. 01 B.5.7 write estimate (751k/day, 37 per application) is superseded by 1.11M/day, ~55 per application (06 0.2).
- Three incompatible connection budgets: 01 B.4.2 (self-run PgBouncer, 108 of 200), 06 X14 (Railway-managed, 134 of 200 growth), 02-database 4.4 (178 of 200). 06 asks 02 to adopt its numbers; 02 is not yet edited. Per-role pool sizes (15/25/4) are not configurable in the Railway-managed PgBouncer, so the isolation intent of 01 is lost.
- Spec-vs-spec scope conflict on P1/P2: 06 12.1 moves the money journal, erasure and tombstones into P2 and observability/backup tooling into P1 while 01 B.14 lists them under P6/P1 bullets without durations. Effort for P1 is not re-estimated after 06 added Grafana, pgbackup, alert-evaluator, IaC and R2 setup.
- Resume-suite PRD D7 (server side on today's Express with migrations 0003+, BullMQ untouched) vs ADR-001 (P3 ports parse/tailor to DBOS, migrations 0003-0012 reserved in 02-database): migration numbers, the parse-resume worker home, and the order R1-R4 vs P0-P8 are undefined. R2 needs Typst in the image (P3 item) and R2 buckets (P1 item) before either phase exists.
- BETTER_AUTH_URL meaning is contradictory: .env.example says web origin (browser reaches API via /api rewrite), 06 4.2 and 05-api 5.1 say public API origin, decision deferred to spike S-B1. This is exactly the deployed gap on sd1-api; nothing says which value to set now.
- Railway claims marked [V] or [verify] carry design weight but are unverified: maximum RAILWAY_DEPLOYMENT_DRAINING_SECONDS (180 needed), IaC helper API (railway/iac), sealed variables with preserve(), R2 token scoping per bucket vs per prefix (01 says prefix, 06 says five buckets), static outbound IP, private IPv6-only networking, whether log retention is adequate. S-INF1 owns them but sits inside the already-overloaded P1.
- Health semantics unclear for worker/applier: Railway healthcheck runs only at deploy and from healthcheck.railway.app; 06 says internal /health on PORT with 120 s timeout, but private services with no domain may not receive the check; no liveness mechanism after deploy is defined except heartbeats.
- 01 gives api 60 s drain with 25-30 s wait but no mechanism to stop DBOS dequeue gracefully; DBOS.shutdown() semantics for in-flight steps and whether it blocks are unspecified (06 1.6 step 1 assumes 'queue listeners stop' exists).
- Local/CI test environment is contradictory: 06 requires PG18 Testcontainers; the repo's tests use pgvector/pgvector:pg17 via TEST_DATABASE_URL and the spike used PG17; spec states the integration suite on PG18 is untested.
- The spec has no concrete design for rolling back a DBOS version bump when old-version workflows already forked, nor for the order of api migration vs worker/applier start (Railway deploys the three services without guaranteed order; 06 3.4 relies on expand-only plus a promotion workflow that does not exist).

**Open questions**

- Approve ADR-001 revision 2 (postgres-durable on DBOS 5.3.0) as Accepted, with the explicit pg-boss fallback trigger if S-D1 (Railway half) fails? Without this, P1 cannot start.
- Do the resume-suite releases R1-R4 ship on today's BullMQ/Redis server (as PRD D7 says) or wait for P3's DBOS parse/tailor? Who owns migration numbers 0003+?
- What are BETTER_AUTH_URL and WEB_ORIGIN supposed to be on sd1-api (Vercel domain vs API origin), and what are they set to today (env.ts requires them, so 'unset' cannot boot)? Which of Apple, GitHub and LinkedIn sign-in must work in the beta, since only Google is configured?
- Which DATABASE_URL do the sd1 api/worker services use today: PgBouncer (transaction mode) or Postgres direct? What Postgres major version and is PITR on?
- Managed Railway PgBouncer (06 X1) or the self-run service with per-role pools (01)?
- Is there a production Railway environment planned before the paid beta, and is the staging-only deploy acceptable for real users in the meantime?
- Paging channel and response expectation for a solo operator (push + phone call ~$25/month, 15-minute response at any hour), or restrict Autopilot/cloud applying to hours you are reachable until a second person exists?
- Accept running Chromium with --no-sandbox under compensating controls if S-AP4 shows it cannot sandbox on Railway, or require another runtime?
- Confirm Sentry Team, Grafana on Railway, Cloudflare R2 (five buckets), Postmark two streams, and an external uptime monitor as new sub-processors/vendors with their monthly cost (~$578-782 platform total).
- Confirm region US East only for the paid beta (data residency of EU/India users) and the 28-day backup erasure tail, pending counsel.
- Confirm release-branch promotion (main to staging, release to production) and manual promote.yml as the only production deploy path.
- Confirm the DOCX library and Typst template licensing for resume-suite R2 (PRD leaves library choice to the builder; D7 only approves the bucket and Typst).
- Static outbound IP and a contactable bot identity for polling/applier: yes or no?
- Who holds the second copy of DATA_KEKS and the backup decryption key (emergency kit), and who can act if you are unreachable?

## resume

Today's server serves only the 2026-10-23 workspace contract: GET/PUT /api/resume, POST /api/resume/suggestions, POST /api/jobs/import, GET /api/jobs/:id/fit, POST /api/jobs/:id/tailor and POST /api/applications/:id/drafts. They run synchronously on the skeleton: code-only truth checks, in-memory 10-a-minute limiters, a heading-parser F1 and no daily allowances. Almost everything else is missing. Not built at all: PDF, DOCX and Typst rendering, R2 storage, file upload and import, revision routes, claim-verifier and jd-analyst, ai_usage enforcement, and all of PRD R1 to R4 (the multi-resume model, templates, the tracker, contacts, interviews and insights). The QA mock (web/scripts/qa/mock-api.mjs) is not ahead of the server on the workspace contract: both cover the same 7 routes. For PRD routes the mock has nothing, the shared zod schemas have no R1 to R4 types, and the web has no /resumes or /tracker routes. D7 says web first against the mock, so the contract and mock are the first blocker for R1. The import worker (workers/11) is built on DBOS and does not exist; the live pipeline is the BullMQ job jobs/parse-resume.ts, which deletes confirmed facts on re-parse, moves onboarding backward and sends unredacted resume text to the model. Worker 11, worker 05 and the PRD contradict each other in several places (spec gaps section).
