**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] AI cost and abuse unbounded by plan allowances: server/src/db/schema/platform.ts:115 ai_usage unused; routes/resume.ts:26 in-memory 10/min limiter (per replica, resets on restart); suggestions/tailor/drafts run Sonnet-class calls. Fix: Ship consumeAllowance/refund (resume-23) and a shared limiter before any wider launch; at minimum move the limiter to Redis (already present for BullMQ).
- [high/confirmed] Truth lock rests on digit and dictionary-skill checks only: server/src/services/invented-details.ts:11-21; no claim-verifier agent; PUT /resume has no claim check (routes/resume.ts:89-97). Fix: Build layer 2 checkClaims (entities, URLs, scope words) and the claim-verifier before R1 expands the number of lines the AI writes; until then keep AI output behind explicit accept (already true) and add layer 2 to PUT and PATCH applications.
- [high/confirmed] Parse job destroys confirmed facts and moves onboarding backward: server/src/jobs/parse-resume.ts:14,16; queues.ts:41 attempts 3. Fix: Rewrite as the S7 write contract (conflict-do-nothing inserts, delete only unconfirmed imports, forward-only step) before adding any re-parse or multi-import (R1). Latent today, live the moment R1 lands.
- [high/confirmed] Raw resume text, including contact, visa and salary lines, goes to the model: server/src/jobs/parse-resume.ts:12 -> services/resume-parser.ts:22/31 (no redaction); llm/redact.ts has no sensitive-line scrub. Fix: Implement redactResumeForParse with fail-closed residue check before enabling file import; it also gates the privacy/ZDR launch claim.
- [high/confirmed] R1/R2 infrastructure is wholly absent and is the critical path: No R2 client, upload parser, extraction thread, Typst, DOCX lib, templates or Dockerfile support (server/package.json, server/Dockerfile). Fix: Sequence as: contract + mock, R2 module, resumes migration, import pipeline, renderer. Treat Typst plus DOCX plus six templates as an XL block and consider shipping R2 with one template first.
- [medium] Typst and Chromium memory on the api/applier images: workers/05 9.2: heap 768 MB + 2 x 256 MB renders exceeds the 1 GB limit; Dockerfile has no typst. Fix: Render in the worker service (not the api) or raise the api limit; the PRD's approval-time render (resume-87) also argues for worker-side rendering.
- [medium] Two parallel designs for resumes, revisions and export: Worker 05 (one resume per slot, /resume/revisions, /resume/pdf) vs PRD (many per profile, /resumes/:id/..., /export); PRD 14 states the divergence. Fix: Declare the PRD authoritative for these tables/routes now, and rewrite worker 05 sections 3.1-3.3, 3.9 against it before building revisions or export, so work is not done twice.
- [medium] Imported jobs from arbitrary boards become actively polled companies: server/src/routes/jobs.ts:24-37 active:true; spec requires false. Fix: Decide the policy; if kept active, cap per-user imports and require the board to be on a verified list, because each import enlarges the polling set permanently.
- [medium] Import writes unscored 'new' matches and 'skipped' failed-gate rows: server/src/routes/jobs.ts:59-68, services/job-import.ts:80. Fix: Align with matches_failed_gate/new_scored invariants when matching tables change; add backfill B-10.
- [medium] Revision history is lossy and unnamed: routes/resume.ts:114-122 writes the new document and keeps 50. Fix: Do snapshot-before-change with coalescing and keep named revisions, as PRD 6.1 requires, before the History UI ships.
- [medium] Sent-snapshot PDFs conflict with the 30-day render lifecycle: workers/05 3.9 renders/ prefix lifecycle 30 days unused; PRD 12 applications.resume_file_key. Fix: Store snapshots under a separate prefix/bucket without expiry (or until account erasure), and keep the cache prefix expiring.
- [medium] Frozen filler change needs CORS/CSP-safe file fetch and the extension has no store release path: extension/src/filler.ts:40-43 fetches resumePdfUrl from the page; cloud-apply.ts:63 never sends it. Fix: Fetch the bytes in the applier process and pass them to Playwright setInputFiles for the cloud path; only the extension path needs the URL.
- [low] Dependence on other sessions' files: PRD 14: routes/profile.ts, services/submissions.ts, lib/auth.ts owned elsewhere; git status shows broad uncommitted changes. Fix: Use new route/service files (routes/resumes.ts, routes/tracker.ts) and mount them in routes/index.ts only.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] Sensitive resume lines and contact data sent to the LLM provider unredacted: server/src/jobs/parse-resume.ts:12; server/src/services/resume-parser.ts:22,35 (resume.slice(0,20000)); server/src/llm/redact.ts has no sensitive-line scrub. Fix: Implement S4 redaction with an offset map and fail-closed residue check; add the seeded-pattern unit test the spec requires.
- [medium] Resume source text stored in plaintext; no envelope encryption or access scoping for files: server/src/db/schema/profile.ts:31 source_text text NOT NULL; file_key unused. Fix: AES-256-GCM with row-bound AAD (DATA_KEKS) when uploads ship; migrate existing text.
- [high/confirmed] Untrusted file parsing not yet designed in code (zip bomb, hostile PDF, macro DOCX): No upload route, parser or sandbox exists; express has only json 1mb (app.ts:33). Fix: Build the capped worker-thread extractor, magic-byte and DOCX entry/size/macro checks, and a fuzz corpus before accepting any file; never parse in the api request thread.
- [medium] Import fetch lacks size cap, address check and redirect control: server/src/services/ats/types.ts:64-72 getJson: global fetch, 15 s timeout, res.json() unbounded, default redirect following. Fix: Route through a safeFetchJson with allowedHosts, 2 MB stream cap, blocked-address check and redirect: 'error'. Host/token are already constrained by parseJobUrl, which limits SSRF risk today.
- [medium] Facts, notes and resume lines are interpolated into prompts without fences on the legacy path: server/src/services/resume-writer.ts:105,226,230 (only note and job use untrusted()); draft-writer.ts:61 factList plain. Fix: Fence facts (T2) and lines; facts can originate from pasted resume text (import) and are attacker-influenced data. Output validation limits blast radius but fencing is the spec.
- [medium] User-edited resume lines and accepted suggestions are not claim-checked before reaching an employer: routes/resume.ts:92-97 (ids only); routes/applications.ts:63-73. Fix: Layer 2 on PUT diff lines and on PATCH applications resumeLines.
- [medium] No per-user quota on uploads/imports or per-replica-safe limiter: routes/jobs.ts:72 importLimiter in memory; no upload class. Fix: Postgres or Redis limiter (10 uploads/hour) before the upload route is enabled.
- [low] Contacts and tracker notes will hold third-party PII and private user text: PRD 9.4, rule 12 (not built). Fix: Per-user scoping in every query, hard delete, account-erasure cascade, and an automated check that prompt builders never select these tables.
- [medium] Presigned/public R2 URLs for resume files: PRD 7.6 (resumePdfUrl) and worker 05 renders/{userId}/ keys. Fix: Short-lived presigned GET only, key not guessable, Cache-Control private no-store, erase by user prefix on account deletion.
- [low] PUT /resume has no concurrency control: routes/resume.ts:99-124 upsert without version check or profile lock. Fix: Add version/ETag and lockProfile; last-write-wins loses an edit silently.

**Spec gaps**

- Allowance error code conflicts: workers/05 3.0/4 and 5.3 say 429 rate_limited with details.reason=daily_allowance; resume-workspace README 9 and PRD 11 say 402 (F11). Pick one; web must handle it.
- Resume cardinality conflict: workers/05 and 02-database use one resumes row per profile slot (resumes_user_slot_uq); PRD 6.1/12 uses many resumes per profile, base plus tailored, with its own columns. PRD 14 admits it but 02-database, 05-api and worker 05 are not amended, so no single schema is buildable.
- Route shape conflict: workers/05 has /api/resume, /resume/pdf, /resume/revisions; PRD 11 has /resumes, /resumes/:id/export, /resumes/:id/revisions. The PRD says the old routes stay as aliases but does not say which resume an alias revisions/pdf call targets.
- Revision policy conflict: worker 05 prunes at 100 plus named plus referenced with 10-minute coalescing and reasons save/pre_ai/restore; PRD keeps 50 unnamed plus named with reasons edit/named/before_ai/before_tailor/restore.
- Import intake conflict: worker 11 uses POST /api/profile/resumes/upload and one source per resume_profiles row with parse state on that row; PRD 6.4/11 uses POST /profile/imports with a resume_imports table so one profile can hold several sources and re-parse replaces only that import's unconfirmed facts. Worker 11's claim/fence/idempotency keys and unique indexes (import_key, source_span_hash) do not account for several imports.
- Worker 11 is written for DBOS (workflow ids, queue parse, R-3 reconciler) but PRD D7 builds R1 on today's Express/BullMQ server. No spec says how claim, attempts, stuck-parse reset and notification work on BullMQ.
- Worker 11 and agent 01 disagree: single call with character offsets and 10-page/9,000-token limit vs line-numbered windows (up to 4), documentKind, datesText, not_a_resume/no_facts codes; Jaccard 0.8 (worker) vs 0.9 (agent); worker returns failures as values vs agent 7.4 throws so DBOS retries.
- DOCX round-trip is required by PRD 7.4 but only PDF/unpdf is specified; no extractor, tolerance or fixture set is defined for DOCX. Page-count parity between preview, Typst and DOCX (A4 vs Letter) is not defined.
- The PRD allows multi-page output and levels' page-length checks, but worker 05 sizes memory, timeouts and permits for a one-page render only; no limits for 3-page documents or long histories.
- Sent snapshot file retention vs renders/ 30-day unused lifecycle is unspecified; so is who renders the snapshot (api vs worker) and how the applier gets bytes from R2.
- Resume check score appears on library cards (PRD 6.6) but is computed in the browser (README 2.4); no server value, field or cache is defined for ResumeSummary.
- Agent specs missing for resume-writer, resume-tailor, claim-verifier, claim-verifier-escalation, jd-analyst, fit-scorer, answer-drafter, cover-letter-writer; worker 05 depends on all (only agents/01-resume-parser.md exists). Addition A10 postValidate rules have no home.
- workers/05 drafts: 'profile question' list (name, email, phone, links, citizenship) and Greenhouse compliance/demographic structure rules are referenced but classifySensitive's contract for them is not written; abstain output schema for answer-drafter is not defined.
- No spec says what the web does with an imported match that has null score ('new' with no score) or how /matches shows manual_url matches (selection=import).
- PRD D9 changes the frozen filler for a file name but the filler already takes resumePdfUrl and the cloud applier never supplies it; the spec assumes an existing upload path that is not wired.
- PRD says tailor 'now saves the copy' for POST /jobs/:jobId/tailor aliases while worker 05 and the 10-23 README say nothing is saved; cache semantics (tailor_cache) vs saved copy are not reconciled.
- Time zone and recurrence handling for interviews and .ics (floating vs UTC) is not specified; 'No reply in 14 days' nudge ownership (server vs web) is not stated.
- Import into a profile when the user is over the resume-profile plan limit: 'creating a new profile stays plan-gated' but importing when no profile slot is free is not defined.
- No README.md master for the backend spec, so the order of P3 deliverables for this domain (migration, allowances, renderer) is only implicit in worker 05 section 12.

**Open questions**

- Which design is authoritative for resumes/revisions/export now: worker 05 (single resume per slot, /resume/*) or the PRD (/resumes/*)? Recommended: PRD, with worker 05 rewritten.
- Should a spent daily AI allowance answer 402 or 429?
- Should imported companies be active=false (spec: not polled until owner activates) or active=true (today's code, so closure is detected)?
- Is rendering done in the api, the worker service, or the applier image? Memory budget, Railway limits and the approval-time render in PRD 7.6 depend on it.
- Which Typst templates ship first? Is one template (Classic) plus DOCX an acceptable R2 cut, with the other five later?
- May R2 hold sent-snapshot PDFs indefinitely (until account deletion), given the privacy notice and the 30-day original-upload retention?
- Is the claim-verifier (third truth-lock layer) a launch gate for R1 import and R3 headline, or can code-only layers 1 and 2 ship first?
- Should PUT /resume and PATCH /applications run layer 2 on user-typed lines (blocking a user's own wording), or only on AI-originated text?
- What is the production plan for WORKSPACE_API and LLM_GATEWAY: when does the legacy chatJson path get deleted?
- For D8, if a user over their slot limit imports a second file, does it merge into the current profile silently or require an upgrade prompt?
- Who owns services/submissions.ts and routes/profile.ts changes (snapshot at approval, upload route)? PRD says another session; confirm sequencing.
- Should the Resume check score be stored server-side for library cards and Insights, or recomputed in the browser only?
- Do the owner-approved R1 import limits (5 MB, 10 pages, 40k chars) apply to the LinkedIn PDF export, which is often longer?

**Capacity**

```json
{
  "currentCapacity": "Single api replica path: synchronous Sonnet calls limited only by an in-memory 10 per minute per user limiter. Parse: BullMQ concurrency 2 on the worker, text only, about 12 s per parse.",
  "assumptions": "10,000 users, 3,500 daily active, about 44,000 workspace requests a day and about 15,300 model calls a day (workers/05 section 4); about 300 parses a day, 1,100 on launch day (workers/11 section 9).",
  "perTier": [
    "api: workspace routes average under 0.02 vCPU; PUT autosave about 0.7 per second peak",
    "worker: parse needs about 200 MB extra per concurrent parse (128 MB extraction heap cap)",
    "renderer: 750 Typst renders a day plus DOCX at about 0.8 s CPU, 60 to 120 MB RSS each; approval-time renders add one per submitted application (about 20,000 a day at growth), which is far more than the 750 the worker 05 sizing assumes",
    "Postgres: about 0.5 writes per second for workspace tables; resume_revisions 1 to 5 GB at 12 months"
  ],
  "cliffs": [
    "Per-application PDF render at approval (resume-87) multiplies renders from about 750 to about 20,000 a day; dedupe by content hash only helps when documents repeat",
    "DOCX plus six templates times two page sizes multiplies the golden/round-trip test matrix",
    "In-memory limiters scale with replica count; allowances become the only real bound",
    "Launch-day parse peak hits the resume-parser $1 daily envelope (workers/11 13.5)"
  ],
  "loadTestPlan": "k6 against suggestions/tailor/drafts with the Anthropic stub for allowance accounting; a render soak of 1,000 fixture resumes per template with round-trip assertions and RSS capture; a malformed-file fuzz corpus through the extractor with memory and time limits; 100 scripted parses with a worker kill.",
  "realityCheck": "Nothing in the renderer, import or tracker path exists, so none of these numbers are measured; only the synchronous workspace routes can be load tested today.",
  "sloProposal": [
    "PUT /resume p95 at most 150 ms",
    "suggestions p95 at most 14 s (line, entry), 30 s (resume)",
    "PDF p95 at most 2.5 s on a miss, 300 ms on a hit",
    "95 percent of parses terminal within 45 s"
  ]
}
```

## scale

Today's Express+BullMQ stack has no load test, no latency metrics and no SLO plumbing, and three unbounded cost/capacity paths: score-matches loops every user with preferences per new job (no pre-rank, no daily caps, N+1 queries), the API limiter is in-memory and keyed by IP behind a proxy, and the cloud applier is one shared Chromium at concurrency 2. The target spec (DBOS on Postgres) is internally inconsistent on its own capacity numbers: connections 108/118/134 of 200, checkpoint writes 751k/1.11M/1.33M a day, api 0.5 vs 1 vCPU, Postmark $120 vs $260. At the maximum replica counts in 06 section 1.3 the connection budget is about 194 of 200. A realistic 10K/100K/1M-user plan is about 150 / 1,000 / 8,300 peak API requests a second, about 800 / 8,000 / 80,000 Anthropic requests a minute at peak, and about $5k / $50-55k / $0.5M a month, of which 80-85% is model spend. The ask of 10K-1M requests per millisecond (10M-1B/s) is 1,200x to 120,000x the 1M-user peak and, taken literally, is the traffic of Cloudflare's whole network (about 60M/s) or more, so it is not a sensible target. Per minute (167-16,700/s) it matches the 10K-1M user range almost exactly, so the unit is probably a slip and needs owner confirmation.

**Risks**

- [high/confirmed] Scoring fan-out cost and time are unbounded: server/src/jobs/score-matches.ts:30-70; cost-model s5 row A: 756k calls/day, $206-413/day at 10k users; one worker replica saturates at about 2,300 users with preferences; no daily caps, LLM_GATEWAY defaults to legacy (env.ts:48). Fix: Ship scale-11/12 on the current stack now (set-based prefilter, dailyScoringCap, activity gating); flip LLM_GATEWAY to anthropic before any real traffic.
- [high/confirmed] No measurement exists: no latency metrics, SLO data or load test: only pino-http logs; no metrics_minute; no k6/fake-ats; S-INF5 scheduled at P8 after the paid-beta gate. Fix: Add route-group latency histograms and queue-lag gauges in P1; run a short k6 + stub smoke at P1 and P4, full S-INF5 before paid beta.
- [high/confirmed] API limiter may throttle all web users as one client: app.ts:22 trust proxy 1 behind Vercel rewrite and Railway edge; rate-limit.ts:7-12 120/min keyed by IP, in memory. Fix: Verify the observed req.ip from the Vercel domain (S-INF2); key by session user after auth; move to the Postgres store (scale-21/22).
- [medium] Spec capacity numbers contradict each other, so there is no single plan: connections 108 (01 B.4.2) vs 118/134 (06 9.2); checkpoint writes 751k (01 B.5.7) vs 1.11M (06 9.3) vs about 1.33M (workers/06 s9); api 0.5 vs 1 vCPU; Postmark $120 vs $260; cost $0.0035 vs $0.0079. Fix: Write one master capacity table (the missing README) and mark cost-model.md superseded for prices and roster.
- [medium] Max replica counts break the Postgres connection budget: 06 1.3 allows api 6/worker 8/applier 16; with 06 9.2 formulas: pooled 60+6, DBOS direct 24+48+48=120, ops 8 = about 194 of 200. Fix: Cap replica maxima by connection math, set PgBouncer pools per role, keep alert at 160.
- [high/confirmed] Single Postgres primary carries business writes, checkpoints, telemetry and rate limits: 06 9.2 peak CPU 1.3x headroom at 10k users; checkpoint peak 52-60 w/s vs 400 trigger; no HA (06 5.8, risk I2). Fix: Set the A.7 second-Postgres trigger on measured peak CPU, not user count; rehearse restore before paid beta.
- [high/confirmed] Anthropic is a hard external ceiling and cost driver: 06 9.5: $4,100/mo vs $1,000 Build cap; 800 RPM peak vs 2,000 requested; single provider by owner decision (risk R8); static per-process buckets (limiter.ts:21-30). Fix: Apply for Scale/custom tier now; derive per-replica RPM from the account limit and replica count; keep shed levels live (scale-16).
- [high/confirmed] Greenhouse polling headroom is only 1.25x and today has no ETag: workers/01 s4.3; poll-boards.ts:49-66 refetches 552k full boards a day (about 138 GB ingress, about 41M conflict checks). Fix: Implement conditional GET and per-host concurrency before adding companies beyond 2,000.
- [medium] Cloud applier fragility caps throughput and strands credits: cloud-apply.ts:18-23 shared Chromium never reset; no sweeper for submitting (submissions.ts:52-58); concurrency 2 means about 11 replicas for 21.5 peak contexts. Fix: Add disconnected handler, run timeout and the lease sweeper before scaling replicas.
- [high/uncertain] Owner ask of 10K-1M requests per millisecond would drive scope and cost if taken literally: 10M-1B req/s is 1,200x-120,000x the 1M-user peak (about 8,300 rps); bandwidth alone about $4M-$390M a month. Fix: Replace with product-derived targets (see capacity.sloProposal) and confirm the unit with the owner.
- [medium] DBOS migration (XL) is the critical path and D7 builds new features on the legacy stack: scale-05 missing; resume-suite D7 ships R1-R4 on Express. Fix: Define interim capacity limits for R1-R4 (render concurrency, allowances) and decide whether scale fixes land before P1.
- [medium] Hard-filter pass rate and cache-hit assumptions are unmeasured: cost-model s2.2 3% pass rate; 01 R15; scoring cost scales linearly with it. Fix: Log pass rate and cache-read share in beta and re-run the model at P8.
- [medium] Free-tier sign-up farming multiplies spend: Free spend about $0.0089 per eligible user per day plus 25 free credits; auth limiter in-memory (auth.ts:69). Fix: Keep the eligibility gate (onboarded, active in 7 days), enforce per-user pipeline cap and Postgres-backed signup limits.

**Security findings**

- [high/confirmed] LLM cost abuse: daily allowances not enforced, limits per replica in memory: routes/resume.ts:26-33,129 writerLimiter 10/min/user per replica; plans.ts:32,68 allowances defined but agents/types.ts:99 says enforced from P3; budgets only on gateway path (env.ts:48 legacy default). Fix: At 10/min a verified account can drive about 14,400 Sonnet calls a day; enforce aiDailyAllowance and per-user spend cap now, turn on the gateway budget.
- [high/confirmed] Unauthenticated 50 MB JSON parse on the inbound-email webhook: routes/webhooks.ts:71 express.json({limit:'50mb'}) runs before the token check at :73; mounted ahead of apiLimiter (app.ts:31-34). Fix: Authenticate first (path secret/Basic), cap concurrent large bodies to one permit per replica, return 503 Retry-After.
- [medium] Per-replica in-memory rate limits (api, auth, OTP email): rate-limit.ts:7-19; lib/auth.ts:69 max 30/60 s; limits multiply by replicas and reset on deploy. Fix: Postgres-backed store shared by all replicas; add per-email and per-IP OTP send limits to avoid Postmark bill and mail bombing.
- [medium] IP-only limiter is both self-DoS and easy to bypass: rate-limit.ts:7-12 key defaults to req.ip with trust proxy 1 (app.ts:22). Fix: Limit authenticated traffic per user, unauthenticated per true client IP with verified hop count.
- [medium] No request timeouts or statement timeouts: app.ts:20-38 no timeout middleware; db/client.ts:13 no statement_timeout; pool max 10. Fix: Per-request deadline, statement_timeout per role, keepAliveTimeout/headersTimeout tuned for the Railway proxy.
- [medium] User-driven growth of the polled company list: routes/jobs.ts:25-33 import inserts companies with active:true; 10/min per user in memory. Fix: Cap user-added boards per user per day, expire unpolled imports, rate-limit by shared Postgres store.

**Spec gaps**

- No README.md master: connection budget has three versions (01 B.4.2 108 of 200; 06 9.2 118 base / 134 growth; workers/14 134) and api limit 0.5 vCPU (01 B.4.1) vs 1 vCPU (06 1.2).
- Checkpoint write budget has three versions: 751k/day at 3 fixed writes (01 B.5.7), 1.11M (06 9.3), about 1.33M because 06 omits queueMatch's 219k (workers/06 s9), plus +30k (03), +14k (07), +7k (08). Unit metric 37 vs 55 vs 66 against target 60.
- 01 B.5.3 still uses partitionQueue/concurrency; 06 9.4 and the workers use S-D1 names partitionConcurrency/globalConcurrency. 01 not updated.
- Cost: cost-model.md (OpenRouter, DeepSeek scorer, $0.0035-0.0038 a submission, $1,586/mo model) is declared 'still binding' by 01 B.12 yet differs from 01/06 ($0.0076-0.0079, $4,092/mo model, Anthropic only). Redis still costed there.
- Pre-rank: cost-model C-3 assumes embeddings and pgvector; 01 A.7 says lexical F1 and 'no vector store'. The 60% keep rate and 3% hard-filter pass rate are estimates with no measurement plan before P4.
- API load model (25 avg / 150 peak rps) is 617 requests per daily active user, taken from stack-options 'about 2M requests a day', not built from routes; workers/05 lists about 44,000 workspace requests a day. B.12.4 claims more than 10x api headroom but 5 ms CPU per request gives about 4x on 3 replicas.
- 06 11.3 Postmark $120 (95,000 emails a month) vs workers/12 s9 $260 (213,000 a month, autopilot_hourly 3,500 vs 600 a day); not reconciled in 06. outcome-analyst envelope $0.20 a day stops a Monday at about 500 of 1,000 reports.
- Applier memory per context: 06 1.2 says 600 MB (4.0 GB total, zero headroom); workers/07 says about 400 MB (2.4 GB); cost-model sizes 6 replicas x 3 contexts, 06/07 8 x 4. No agreed number until S-AP1.
- Replica maxima in 06 1.3 (api 6, worker 8, applier 16) are not checked against the connection budget; direct DBOS connections alone reach 120 and the total about 194 of 200.
- SLO set covers availability, read latency and queue lag but has no latency SLO for writes (approve, PATCH, report), auth, webhooks acknowledgement or feed freshness after a poll ('about 10 minutes' in B.12.4 is unmeasured).
- Nothing beyond 50,000 users: no partitioning/sharding, no plan for ATS politeness as a hard ceiling, no Anthropic limits beyond 5x, no capacity numbers for 100k or 1M.
- The resume-suite PRD (R1-R4) has no performance, cost or capacity requirements; D7 puts the server work on today's Express before DBOS exists, with no interim limits for render concurrency or file parsing.
- Load test S-INF5 is scheduled at P8, after the paid-beta gate (P0-P5), so the first paying users arrive before any capacity evidence.

**Open questions**

- What did '10K-1M requests per millisecond' mean? Literally 10M-1B requests a second, or per minute (167-16,700 a second, which matches 10K-1M users)? Please state the unit and whether it is a peak or a sustained figure.
- What is the funded planning target for the next 12 months: 10k, 50k or 1M registered users? At 1M the model bill alone is about $0.4M a month.
- Do you approve SLOs of 99.5% API availability (99.9% for approve, report and webhooks), read p95 300 ms and the queue-lag targets in capacity.sloProposal, given a solo on-call?
- Will you apply for an Anthropic Scale/custom tier now (RPM 2,000+, spend above $1,000), and accept single-provider dependence?
- Should scale fixes (scoring caps, shared rate limiter, ETag polling) land on today's Express stack before the DBOS migration, or wait for P1-P4?
- Is PgBouncer in front of the DATABASE_URL used by sd1-api today, and in which mode? (not verifiable from the repo; .env files were not read)
- Which regions are the users in? A US-only Railway region may not meet a 300 ms p95 for India-heavy users; region decision needed before SLO sign-off.
- Keep 25 free credits and scoring for Free at scale (75% of users, about $0.0089 a day each in model spend), or tighten Free caps?
- Postmark or SES once volume passes about 100,000 emails a month ($260 vs about $25)?
- May we spend on a short Railway staging load test (k6 plus stubs) before the paid-beta gate?

## tests

Unit suite is healthy: 30 files, 332 of 332 tests pass offline in 7.8s (run with DATABASE_URL and TEST_DATABASE_URL unset, integration files excluded; nothing tried to reach a database or the network). About 60 more tests are Postgres integration tests (6 *.int.test.ts files plus contract.int with 33 routes), and they were not run. They are skipped whenever TEST_DATABASE_URL is unset, and .github/workflows/ci.yml has no Postgres service and never sets it, so CI never runs the tests that prove the money and ordering invariants (charge once, approval credit race, Stripe replay, nothing sent before approval, no scoring call after hard-filter failure). CI today is typecheck, lint (web only), npm test (server unit, web, extension), build, and a docker build; the 9-job CI, staging-gate, promote, nightly and evals workflows of 06-infrastructure.md section 3 do not exist. P0 of ADR-001 (contract suite, Testcontainers PG18, invariants, known-defect expected failures) is only partly done: the contract suite checks mounting and auth, not response shapes, and there are no expected-failure tests. P1 gateway unit coverage is good (23 gateway tests, registry, schema, fence, redact, prices) but the budget, limiter, breaker, ledger and partition code has no dedicated tests, and no eval harness, recorded fixtures, authorization matrix, serializer leak tests, SSRF suite, grant tests, load tests or chaos tests exist. The resume-suite PRD acceptance (R1 to R4) has no server-side tests yet, as expected under D7. Biggest release-gate gaps: integration tests in CI, evals with pass bars, an authorization matrix, the grant and role tests (roles are not even provisioned), and the chaos and load suites.

**Risks**

- [critical/confirmed] CI is green while every invariant integration test is skipped: .github/workflows/ci.yml:15-35 (no Postgres, no TEST_DATABASE_URL); server/test/helpers/test-db.ts:16; all six *.int.test.ts use describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb). Fix: Add a postgres service to the checks job (or Testcontainers), set TEST_DATABASE_URL, and make the int run a required check before any P2 work. Pin to the same major as production (spec says 18, staging Railway image should be confirmed).
- [high/confirmed] The only code that submits to employers has no tests: server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:31-74 and server/src/applier.ts untested; extension/test/filler.test.ts only covers jsdom. Fix: Add a fake-ATS Playwright test for processCloudApply (approved, withdrawn mid-flight, filler missing, thrown error, no charge on failure) before paid beta.
- [high/confirmed] Ledger append-only and role isolation unproven: no server/ops/provision-roles.sql; drizzle/0002_platform_02_llm_ledger.sql:4; no grant test. Fix: Provision roles and add T-DB-1 before the credit_balances work; today any code path using the single DB role can update or delete credit_ledger.
- [high/confirmed] No authorization (IDOR) test anywhere: no two-user test in server/test; routes.int.test.ts:45-58 single user. Fix: Write a table-driven two-user test over all owned-resource routes now (before the route registry exists) and extend it for new resume-suite routes.
- [high/confirmed] Truth lock semantic layer untested and unbuilt: no claim-verifier agent; tests cover only deterministic fact-id and invented-number checks (draft-writer.test.ts, resume-writer.test.ts). Fix: Treat the eval set for invented employer, title, date, scope and tool as a prerequisite for Autopilot; do not open Autopilot without it.
- [high/confirmed] No eval harness, so prompt or model changes ship ungated: server/evals absent; no evals.yml; plan says no live Anthropic call has run (phase-a.md Status 2026-10-24). Fix: Build a minimal deterministic eval (schema validity, id membership, number containment, protected-question leakage) first; add rubric graders later.
- [medium] Contract suite cannot detect shape or status drift on frozen routes: server/test/contract.int.test.ts:44-52. Fix: Record request and response fixtures from the QA mock and the live skeleton, assert statuses, error codes and zod parses.
- [medium] Test DB drift: harness comment uses pg17 pgvector, spec requires PG18 with PgBouncer: server/test/helpers/test-db.ts:12; docker-compose.yml:6. Fix: Move to PG18 in the harness now; migrations passing on 17 do not prove 18 (and PgBouncer transaction pooling breaks advisory locks and SET).
- [medium] Dependency ranges not pinned and Dependabot, not Renovate, with no age delay: server/package.json caret ranges; no .npmrc; .github/dependabot.yml. Fix: Add save-exact, a range-fail CI step and a minimum release age, or record the divergence in the spec.
- [medium] No automated post-deploy verification on staging: only ci.yml exists; Railway project kairos-sd1-staging deploys with no gate. Fix: A small staging smoke (health, sign-in with the Vercel origin, GET /api/me) would have caught the unset WEB_ORIGIN and BETTER_AUTH_URL gap.
- [medium] Server has no lint script, so npm run lint does not cover server, shared or extension: server/package.json:6-22; shared/package.json:11; extension/package.json:9-11. Fix: Add ESLint with the module-boundary rules (only llm/gateway imports the Anthropic SDK; only the credits service inserts into credit_ledger) so INV-5 and INV-15 hold structurally.
- [low] Uncommitted work and shared checkout make test results hard to attribute: git status: many modified/untracked paths including shared/src/plans.ts, docs specs. Fix: Commit the test and plans changes in small commits so CI runs on what is described.

**Security findings**

- [medium] No automated secret or dependency scanning in CI: .github/workflows/ci.yml has no gitleaks, osv-scanner, Semgrep, CodeQL or Trivy; only .github/dependabot.yml. Fix: Add gitleaks (diff and history) and osv-scanner first; they are free and quick. Local .env files exist in the repo working tree, so push protection matters.
- [high/confirmed] No cross-user authorization tests (IDOR risk is untested): no multi-user test in server/test. Fix: Add the INV-11 matrix test; queries are meant to filter by session user id (PR template line 14) but nothing proves it.
- [high/confirmed] Append-only credit ledger and least-privilege DB roles are not enforced or tested: drizzle/0002_platform_02_llm_ledger.sql:4 defers grants; no server/ops. Fix: Provision roles and add the grant matrix test.
- [medium] No SSRF tests around job import and board fetches: server/test/job-url.test.ts only parses URLs; no private-IP, redirect or rebinding cases. Fix: Add redirect and private-address cases for fetchPostingFromBoard now; implement the safeFetch suite with P5.
- [medium] No test that a CAPTCHA or bot-check page is detected and left alone (INV-8): server/src/jobs/cloud-apply.ts:49-60 navigates and fills with no challenge check. Fix: Add challenge detection plus a fixture test that the attempt fails with no charge.
- [low] No Stripe wrong-signature test: server/test/webhooks.int.test.ts only sends valid signatures. Fix: Add one test with a signature for a different body expecting 400.
- [medium] No test pinning that EEO, disability, veteran, visa and salary values on the autofill path come only from the user: sensitive.test.ts and routes.int.test.ts:178 cover the drafts route; tailor worker and filler path unverified. Fix: Add a worker-level test that sensitive fields remain empty or profile-sourced after processTailorApplication.

**Spec gaps**

- 01-architecture.md B.14 P0 asks for Testcontainers Postgres 18 but the repo and docker-compose run Postgres 17; the spec does not say whether the local developer path (manual TEST_DATABASE_URL) remains supported.
- 06-infrastructure.md 3.2 says 'vitest run (41 existing tests plus new ones)'; the suite is now 332 unit plus about 60 integration tests, so the count and the 'existing tests green' exit criteria are stale.
- No master README.md, so there is no single list of test ids (AC-n, T-n, T-DB-n) mapped to files; AC-6 to AC-14 are cited in B.16 but their definitions live in a research file (_research/requirements.md) and were not found with test file names.
- 06-infrastructure.md 3.2 calls the integration job 'Testcontainers Postgres 18 with DBOS launched in three roles' as a required PR check, but DBOS is a P1 deliverable; P0 needs a no-DBOS definition of the required check.
- 03-agent-runtime.md 15.3 lists test/gateway-spy.test.ts as an integration test but also requires zero runAgent calls inside privileged paths (11.5) with no mechanism named (AST lint or runtime spy).
- Eval pass bars (B.9.10) name case counts (40 'offer you an interview', 60 hardest verifier cases, 100 retrieval cases, 500 scorer pairs) but no spec says who labels them or where synthetic personas live beyond server/evals/shared/personas.json.
- The spec requires the contract suite to replay 'recorded calls', yet nothing defines the recording format or who records after the frontend changes; 05-api.md 18.6 mentions an extractor but no output schema.
- The PRD resume suite (D7) puts the server on today's Express with migrations 0003+, but test obligations for tracker, DOCX, templates and .ics are only stated as acceptance bullets, with no fixtures or pass bars (for example 'round-trip check' is undefined).
- 06-infrastructure.md 3.2 requires a signed-commit, linear-history branch protection but no document says if it is configured on GitHub.
- B.16 cites 'Code review gate' as the proof for INV-6 (no mailbox reads) with no automatable check, and 'Boot test' for INV-20 with no test described.

**Open questions**

- Is GitHub branch protection (required checks, signed commits, linear history) enabled on main today, and may CI be changed to add a Postgres service now rather than waiting for the Testcontainers/PG18 move?
- Which Postgres major does Railway staging run today (17 or 18)? The test harness and docker-compose use 17.
- Who will supply and label the eval corpora (resumes, postings, sensitive questions, injection cases), and is a budget of about $120 a month for evals approved ahead of P3?
- Is a real Anthropic key available for a first smoke call and for recording LLM fixtures (the plan says no live call has run)?
- Should the 41 'existing tests' exit criterion for P0 be restated against the current suite, and may known-defect tests (D-07, D-08, D-13, D-14) be written as it.fails now?
- Is the order Dependabot to Renovate (14-day minimum age, exact pins) wanted, or should the spec be amended to accept Dependabot?
- For the resume suite R1 to R4 under D7, does the owner want a server-side acceptance test plan written before the migrations (0003+), or after the web release?
- Is an external penetration test (estimated $4,000 to $8,000) approved before public beta, and when should the one-off DAST and upload-parser fuzz start?
- Should the extension's built filler (extension/dist/filler.js, gitignored) be hash-pinned in CI so any change to extension/ fails the build, as INV-16 implies?
